/* ===== FILE: alien-shop.js ===== */
(() => {
  'use strict';

  const STORAGE_KEY = 'starfleet-game-resources-v1';
  const format = value => Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(value) || 0)).toLocaleString('ko-KR');
  const CURRENCIES = {
    gold: { name: '골드', image: 'gold-resource-icon.svg' },
    thorium: { name: '토륨', image: 'thorium-crystal-icon.png' },
    lionToken: { name: '사자증표', image: 'icons/lion-token.png' }
  };
  const TABS = [
    { id: 'featured', label: '오늘 특가!' },
    { id: 'supply', label: '보급품' },
    { id: 'research', label: '토륨 상점' },
    { id: 'black-market', label: '암시장' }
  ];
  const GOODS = [
    { sku: 'arena-ticket', itemId: 'arenaTicket', name: '아레나 티켓', image: 'icons/arena-button.png', detail: '무료 5회 소진 후 자동전투 1회 추가', amount: 1, currency: 'thorium', price: 50, badge: 'ARENA', groups: ['supply'], arenaTicket: true },
    { sku: 'scout-drone', itemId: 'scoutTron', name: '탐색트론', image: 'icons/scout-drone.png', detail: '함선 탐색에 필요한 정찰 장비', amount: 1, currency: 'thorium', price: 100, badge: '탐색 보급', groups: ['featured', 'supply'] },
    { sku: 'scout-drone-ten', itemId: 'scoutTron', name: '탐색트론 묶음', image: 'icons/scout-drone.png', detail: '탐색트론 10개를 한 번에 보급', amount: 10, currency: 'thorium', price: 900, badge: '10개 묶음', groups: ['supply'] },
    { sku: 'research-crew', itemId: 'researchStaff', name: '연구인력', image: 'icons/research-personnel-20261002.png', detail: '전술무기 연구에 투입할 인력', amount: 1, currency: 'thorium', price: 50, badge: '연구 필수', groups: ['featured', 'supply'] },
    { sku: 'research-crew-ten', itemId: 'researchStaff', name: '연구인력 묶음', image: 'icons/research-personnel-20261002.png', detail: '연구인력 10개를 한 번에 보급', amount: 10, currency: 'thorium', price: 450, badge: '10개 묶음', groups: ['supply'] },
  ];
  const TOKEN_PACKS = [1, 5, 10, 20, 25, 50, 100, 200, 500, 1000];
  const THORIUM_OFFERS = [1, 5, 10, 20, 50, 100, 500, 1000].map(amount => ({
    sku: `token-thorium-${amount}`, name: `토륨 ${format(amount * 100)}개`, image: 'icons/lion-token.png',
    detail: `사자증표 ${format(amount)}개 교환 · 1개당 토륨 100개`, amount, currency: 'lionToken', price: amount,
    badge: '사자증표 교환', groups: ['research'], rewardType: 'thorium', rewardAmount: amount * 100
  }));
  const DAILY_KEY = 'starfleet-shop-daily-gift-v1';
  const FEATURED_KEY = 'starfleet-shop-featured-v1';
  const STALL_KEY = 'starfleet-shop-token-stalls-v1';
  const FEATURED_GOODS = [
    ...[1, 5, 10, 20].map((amount, index) => ({
      sku: `daily-scout-${amount}`, itemId: 'scoutTron', name: '탐색드론', image: 'icons/scout-drone.png',
      detail: `토륨 환산가 ${format([10000, 50000, 90000, 180000][index])} 골드 · 20% 할인`, amount, currency: 'gold', price: [8000, 40000, 72000, 144000][index], badge: '20% 할인', groups: ['featured']
    })),
    ...[1, 5, 10, 20].map((amount, index) => ({
      sku: `daily-research-${amount}`, itemId: 'researchStaff', name: '연구인력', image: 'icons/research-personnel-20261002.png',
      detail: `토륨 환산가 ${format([5000, 25000, 45000, 90000][index])} 골드 · 20% 할인`, amount, currency: 'gold', price: [4000, 20000, 36000, 72000][index], badge: '20% 할인', groups: ['featured']
    })),
    ...[50, 100].map((amount, index) => ({
      sku: `daily-thorium-${amount}`, itemId: 'thorium', name: '토륨', image: 'thorium-crystal-icon.png',
      detail: `토륨 ${format(amount)}개 · 기준가 ${format(amount * 100)} 골드 · 20% 할인`, amount, currency: 'gold', price: [4000, 8000][index], badge: '20% 할인', groups: ['featured'], rewardType: 'resource'
    }))
  ];
  const TOKEN_STALLS = Array.from({ length: 6 }, (_, index) => ({ index: index + 1, unlockPair: Math.floor(index / 2) + 1 }));
  const TOKEN_PAIR_UNLOCK_COST = 1;
  const FIXED_KR_HOLIDAYS = new Set(['01-01', '03-01', '05-01', '05-05', '06-06', '07-17', '08-15', '10-03', '10-09', '12-25']);
  const LUNAR_KR_HOLIDAYS = new Set([
    '2026-02-16','2026-02-17','2026-02-18','2026-03-02','2026-05-24','2026-05-25','2026-06-08','2026-08-17','2026-09-24','2026-09-25','2026-09-26','2026-10-05',
    '2027-02-05','2027-02-06','2027-02-07','2027-05-03','2027-05-13','2027-06-07','2027-08-16','2027-09-14','2027-09-15','2027-09-16','2027-10-04','2027-10-11','2027-12-27'
  ]);
  let unlockedTokenPairs = 0;
  let dailyGiftBusy = false;
  const purchaseThankyou = window.createGameAudio('sound/arka_thankyou.ogg');
  purchaseThankyou.preload = 'auto';
  purchaseThankyou.volume = 0.5;
  let overlay = null;
  let activeTab = 'featured';
  let selectedGood = null;
  let lastFocus = null;
  let toastTimer = 0;

  function balances() {
    const current = window.gameResources || (window.gameResources = { gold: 1240, thorium: 86 });
    if (!Number.isFinite(Number(current.lionToken))) current.lionToken = 0;
    if (!window.__inventoryBalancesHydrated) {
      try {
        const saved = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY) || 'null');
        if (saved && typeof saved === 'object') Object.assign(current, saved);
      } catch {}
      window.__inventoryBalancesHydrated = true;
    }
    return current;
  }

  function owned(itemId) {
    if(itemId==='arenaTicket')return window.getArenaTickets?.()||0;
    return Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(window.getExplorationLootInventory?.()?.[itemId]) || 0));
  }

  function currencyMarkup(type, amount) {
    const currency = CURRENCIES[type];
    return `<span class="alien-shop-price ${type}"><img src="${currency.image}" alt="" />${format(amount)}</span>`;
  }

  function tokenPileMarkup(amount) {
    if (amount === 1) return '<img class="alien-shop-token-single" src="icons/lion-token.png" alt="사자증표 1개" />';
    if (amount <= 10) {
      const positions = amount === 5
        ? [[14, 6, -14], [43, 3, 9], [74, 8, -8], [31, 29, 15], [62, 30, -12]]
        : [[4, 7, -18], [32, 2, 12], [59, 8, -15], [88, 3, 10], [17, 22, 16], [46, 27, -11], [75, 23, 20], [2, 36, -8], [41, 36, 8], [87, 35, -17]];
      const coins = positions.map(([x, y, angle]) =>
        `<img src="icons/lion-token.png" alt="" style="left:${x}px;top:${y}px;transform:rotate(${angle}deg)" />`
      ).join('');
      return `<div class="alien-shop-loose-coins ${amount === 10 ? 'scattered' : ''}" role="img" aria-label="사자증표 ${amount}개">${coins}</div>`;
    }
    if (amount === 25) {
      return `<div class="alien-shop-twenty-five" role="img" aria-label="사자증표 20개 더미와 낱개 5개">
        <span class="alien-shop-tray-fragment" style="width:60px;height:60px"><img src="icons/lion-token-pile.png" alt="" style="width:120px" /></span>
        <div class="alien-shop-five-group" aria-hidden="true">${tokenPileMarkup(5)}</div>
      </div>`;
    }
    // The supplied tray depicts approximately 40 tokens; partial trays show the remainder.
    const fullTrays = Math.floor(amount / 40);
    const remainder = amount % 40;
    const trayCount = fullTrays + (remainder ? 1 : 0);
    const columns = trayCount <= 1 ? 1 : trayCount <= 4 ? 2 : trayCount <= 6 ? 3 : trayCount <= 12 ? 4 : 5;
    const traySize = trayCount <= 1 ? 120 : trayCount <= 4 ? 64 : trayCount <= 6 ? 50 : trayCount <= 12 ? 37 : 26;
    const trays = Array.from({ length: trayCount }, (_, index) => {
      const fraction = index < fullTrays ? 1 : remainder / 40;
      return `<span class="alien-shop-tray-fragment" style="width:${(traySize * fraction).toFixed(2)}px;height:${(traySize / 2).toFixed(2)}px"><img src="icons/lion-token-pile.png" alt="" style="width:${traySize}px" /></span>`;
    }).join('');
    return `<div class="alien-shop-tray-grid" role="img" aria-label="사자증표 ${format(amount)}개" style="grid-template-columns:repeat(${columns},${traySize}px)">${trays}</div>`;
  }

  function thoriumRewardImageMarkup(good) {
    if (good.rewardAmount >= 5000) {
      return `<div class="alien-shop-good-art alien-shop-thorium-mountain" role="img" aria-label="토륨 상자 네 줄 적재와 열린 상자 세 개"><img class="alien-shop-thorium-pile-image" src="thorium-chest-mountain-shop.png" alt="" /></div>`;
    }
    if (good.rewardAmount < 500) {
      return `<div class="alien-shop-good-art"><img src="thorium-crystal-icon.png" alt="토륨 결정" /></div>`;
    }
    const boxes = Math.max(1, Math.floor(good.rewardAmount / 500));
    if (boxes === 4) {
      const twoBoxes = '<img src="icons/thorium-box-open.png" alt="" /><img src="icons/thorium-box-open.png" alt="" />';
      return `<div class="alien-shop-good-art alien-shop-thorium-box-stack" aria-label="열린 토륨 상자 4개"><div class="alien-shop-thorium-boxes-four"><span class="rear">${twoBoxes}</span><span class="front">${twoBoxes}</span></div></div>`;
    }
    const images = Array.from({ length: boxes }, (_, index) =>
      `<img src="icons/thorium-box-open.png" alt="" style="--box-index:${index}" />`
    ).join('');
    return `<div class="alien-shop-good-art alien-shop-thorium-box-stack" aria-label="열린 토륨 상자 ${boxes}개">${images}</div>`;
  }

  function cardMarkup(good) {
    const isFeaturedOffer = FEATURED_GOODS.some(offer => offer.sku === good.sku);
    const featuredState = isFeaturedOffer ? dailyFeaturedState() : null;
    const sold = Boolean(featuredState?.bought?.includes(good.sku));
    return `<article class="alien-shop-good ${good.currency === 'thorium' ? 'premium' : ''}">
      ${good.rewardType === 'thorium' ? thoriumRewardImageMarkup(good) : `<div class="alien-shop-good-art"><img src="${good.image}" alt="" /></div>`}
      <span class="alien-shop-good-badge">${good.badge}</span>
      <h3>${good.name}${good.rewardType === 'thorium' ? '' : good.amount > 1 ? `<small>× ${good.amount}</small>` : ''}</h3>
      <p>${good.detail}</p>
      <div class="alien-shop-good-bottom"><span class="alien-shop-owned">보유 ${format(owned(good.itemId))}</span><button type="button" data-shop-sku="${good.sku}" aria-label="${sold ? `${good.name} 판매 완료` : `${good.name} 교역 조건 확인`}" ${sold ? 'disabled' : ''}>${sold ? '오늘 판매 완료' : currencyMarkup(good.currency, good.price)}</button></div>
    </article>`;
  }

  function todayKey() {
    const parts = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA', { timeZone: 'Asia/Seoul', year: 'numeric', month: '2-digit', day: '2-digit' }).formatToParts(new Date());
    const part = type => parts.find(entry => entry.type === type)?.value || '00';
    return `${part('year')}-${part('month')}-${part('day')}`;
  }

  function isKoreanHoliday(dateKey) {
    const date = new Date(`${dateKey}T12:00:00+09:00`);
    const weekday = date.getDay();
    return weekday === 0 || weekday === 6 || FIXED_KR_HOLIDAYS.has(dateKey.slice(5)) || LUNAR_KR_HOLIDAYS.has(dateKey);
  }

  function dailyGiftState() {
    const key = todayKey();
    let state;
    try { state = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(DAILY_KEY) || 'null'); } catch {}
    if (!state || state.date !== key) state = { date: key, claims: 0, rewards: [] };
    return { ...state, date: key, claims: Math.max(0, Number(state.claims) || 0), rewards: Array.isArray(state.rewards) ? state.rewards : [] };
  }

  function dailyFeaturedState() {
    const key = todayKey();
    let state;
    try { state = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(FEATURED_KEY) || 'null'); } catch {}
    if (!state || state.date !== key || !Array.isArray(state.skus)) {
      const shuffled = [...FEATURED_GOODS];
      for (let index = shuffled.length - 1; index > 0; index -= 1) {
        const swap = Math.floor(Math.random() * (index + 1));
        [shuffled[index], shuffled[swap]] = [shuffled[swap], shuffled[index]];
      }
      state = { date: key, skus: shuffled.slice(0, 4).map(good => good.sku), bought: [] };
      try { localStorage.setItem(FEATURED_KEY, JSON.stringify(state)); } catch {}
    }
    return { ...state, bought: Array.isArray(state.bought) ? state.bought : [] };
  }

  function playPurchaseThankyou() {
    try {
      purchaseThankyou.currentTime = 0;
      purchaseThankyou.play()?.catch?.(() => {});
    } catch {}
  }

  function dailyGiftMarkup() {
    const state = dailyGiftState();
    const allowance = isKoreanHoliday(state.date) ? 2 : 1;
    const rewards = [
      { itemId: 'thorium', name: '토륨 50', image: 'thorium-crystal-icon.png', amount: 50 },
      { itemId: 'gold', name: '골드 1,000', image: 'gold-resource-icon.svg', amount: 1000 },
      { itemId: 'scoutTron', name: '탐색드론 1', image: 'icons/scout-drone.png', amount: 1 },
      { itemId: 'researchStaff', name: '연구인력 1', image: 'icons/research-personnel-20261002.png', amount: 1 }
    ];
    const claimed = state.claims >= allowance;
    const rewardRows = state.rewards.map(reward => `<span class="alien-shop-gift-history"><img src="${reward.image}" alt="" />${reward.name}</span>`).join('');
    return `<section class="alien-shop-daily-gift"><div class="alien-shop-gift-copy"><small>DAILY LOGIN GIFT</small><strong>방문 선물</strong><span>무작위 선물 · ${isKoreanHoliday(state.date) ? '주말·공휴일 2회' : '평일 1회'}</span>${rewardRows ? `<div class="alien-shop-gift-history-list">${rewardRows}</div>` : ''}</div><button type="button" data-daily-gift ${claimed || dailyGiftBusy ? 'disabled' : ''}><span>${claimed ? '오늘 수령 완료' : '무료 수령'}</span><small>${state.claims}/${allowance}</small></button></section>`;
  }

  function tokenStallMarkup() {
    const pairs = Array.from({ length: 3 }, (_, index) => ({
      number: index + 1,
      slots: TOKEN_STALLS.slice(index * 2, index * 2 + 2)
    }));
    return `<section class="alien-shop-token-stall"><header><strong>Membership Service</strong></header><div class="alien-shop-stall-grid">${pairs.map(pair => {
      const unlocked = pair.number <= unlockedTokenPairs;
      const canUnlock = !unlocked && pair.number === unlockedTokenPairs + 1 && pair.number === 1;
      return `<section class="alien-shop-stall-pair ${unlocked ? 'unlocked' : 'locked'}" aria-label="${pair.number}번째 판매대 두 칸 ${unlocked ? '개방' : '잠김'}"><div class="alien-shop-stall-pair-slots">${pair.slots.map(slot => `<article class="alien-shop-stall-slot ${unlocked ? 'unlocked' : ''}"><span class="alien-shop-stall-number">SLOT 0${slot.index}</span><span class="alien-shop-stall-icon">${unlocked ? '✧' : '◇'}</span><strong>${unlocked ? '상품 준비 중' : '잠긴 판매대'}</strong></article>`).join('')}</div>${!unlocked ? `<div class="alien-shop-pair-lock" aria-hidden="true"><span>🔒</span><b>두 칸 잠김</b></div>` : ''}<button class="alien-shop-pair-action" type="button" data-token-unlock="${pair.number}" ${unlocked || !canUnlock ? 'disabled' : ''}>${unlocked ? '2칸 개방 완료' : canUnlock ? `2칸 개방 · ${format(TOKEN_PAIR_UNLOCK_COST)}개` : '후속 조건 대기'}</button></section>`;
    }).join('')}</div><p>사자증표 보유한 사람만 거래할 수 있습니다.</p></section>`;
  }

  function claimDailyGift() {
    if (dailyGiftBusy || !overlay) return;
    const state = dailyGiftState();
    const allowance = isKoreanHoliday(state.date) ? 2 : 1;
    if (state.claims >= allowance) return;
    dailyGiftBusy = true;
    const rewards = [
      { itemId: 'thorium', name: '토륨 50', image: 'thorium-crystal-icon.png', amount: 50 },
      { itemId: 'gold', name: '골드 1,000', image: 'gold-resource-icon.svg', amount: 1000 },
      { itemId: 'scoutTron', name: '탐색드론 1', image: 'icons/scout-drone.png', amount: 1 },
      { itemId: 'researchStaff', name: '연구인력 1', image: 'icons/research-personnel-20261002.png', amount: 1 }
    ];
    const reward = rewards[Math.floor(Math.random() * rewards.length)];
    const current = balances();
    const isInventory = reward.itemId === 'scoutTron' || reward.itemId === 'researchStaff';
    if (isInventory && !window.grantExplorationLoot?.(reward.itemId, reward.amount)) {
      dailyGiftBusy = false;
      showToast('선물을 지급하지 못했습니다. 다시 시도해 주세요.');
      return;
    }
    if (reward.itemId === 'gold' || reward.itemId === 'thorium') current[reward.itemId] = (Number(current[reward.itemId]) || 0) + reward.amount;
    state.claims += 1;
    state.rewards.push({ name: reward.name, image: reward.image });
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(current));
      localStorage.setItem(DAILY_KEY, JSON.stringify(state));
    } catch {
      dailyGiftBusy = false;
      showToast('수령 기록을 저장하지 못했습니다. 다시 시도해 주세요.');
      return;
    }
    dailyGiftBusy = false;
    window.updateGameResourceHud?.();
    window.dispatchEvent(new Event('game-resources-updated'));
    render();
    showToast(`방문 선물: ${reward.name} 획득!`);
  }

  function unlockTokenPair(pair) {
    const current = balances();
    if (pair !== unlockedTokenPairs + 1 || pair !== 1) return;
    if (Number(current.lionToken) < TOKEN_PAIR_UNLOCK_COST) {
      showToast('사자증표가 부족합니다.');
      return;
    }
    current.lionToken -= TOKEN_PAIR_UNLOCK_COST;
    unlockedTokenPairs = pair;
    try { localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(current)); localStorage.setItem(STALL_KEY, String(unlockedTokenPairs)); } catch {
      current.lionToken += TOKEN_PAIR_UNLOCK_COST;
      unlockedTokenPairs = pair - 1;
      showToast('판매대 개방을 저장하지 못했습니다.');
      return;
    }
    window.updateGameResourceHud?.();
    window.dispatchEvent(new Event('game-resources-updated'));
    render();
    showToast('Membership Service 첫 두 칸을 개방했습니다.');
  }

  function cashCardMarkup(amount) {
    return `<article class="alien-shop-good premium alien-shop-cash-good">
      <div class="alien-shop-good-art">${tokenPileMarkup(amount)}</div>
      <span class="alien-shop-good-badge">암시장 · 현금 상품</span>
      <h3>사자증표 <small>× ${format(amount)}</small></h3>
      <p>개당 ₩1,500 · 결제 연동 준비 중</p>
      <div class="alien-shop-good-bottom"><span class="alien-shop-owned">₩${format(amount * 1500)}</span><button type="button" data-cash-pack="${amount}">상품 보기</button></div>
    </article>`;
  }

  function render() {
    if (!overlay) return;
    const current = balances();
    overlay.querySelector('[data-shop-balance="gold"]').textContent = format(current.gold);
    overlay.querySelector('[data-shop-balance="thorium"]').textContent = format(current.thorium);
    overlay.querySelector('[data-shop-balance="lionToken"]').textContent = format(current.lionToken);
    overlay.querySelectorAll('[data-shop-tab]').forEach(button => {
      const selected = button.dataset.shopTab === activeTab;
      button.classList.toggle('active', selected);
      button.setAttribute('aria-selected', String(selected));
    });
    const goods = GOODS.filter(good => good.groups.includes(activeTab));
    const todaysFeaturedGoods = dailyFeaturedState().skus.map(sku => FEATURED_GOODS.find(good => good.sku === sku)).filter(Boolean);
    const markup = activeTab === 'black-market'
      ? TOKEN_PACKS.map(cashCardMarkup).join('')
      : activeTab === 'research' ? THORIUM_OFFERS.map(cardMarkup).join('')
      : activeTab === 'featured' ? `${dailyGiftMarkup()}<section class="alien-shop-featured-shelf"><header><strong>오늘의 보급품</strong></header><div class="alien-shop-featured-grid">${todaysFeaturedGoods.map(cardMarkup).join('')}</div></section>${tokenStallMarkup()}`
      : goods.map(cardMarkup).join('');
    overlay.querySelector('.alien-shop-grid').innerHTML = markup;
    overlay.querySelector('.alien-shop-section-title').textContent = TABS.find(tab => tab.id === activeTab)?.label || '오늘 특가!';
    const sectionCount = overlay.querySelector('.alien-shop-section-count');
    sectionCount.hidden = activeTab === 'featured';
    sectionCount.textContent = activeTab === 'featured' ? '' : `${activeTab === 'black-market' ? TOKEN_PACKS.length : activeTab === 'research' ? THORIUM_OFFERS.length : goods.length}개 상품`;
  }

  function showToast(message) {
    if (!overlay) return;
    const toast = overlay.querySelector('.alien-shop-toast');
    clearTimeout(toastTimer);
    toast.textContent = message;
    toast.classList.add('visible');
    toastTimer = setTimeout(() => toast.classList.remove('visible'), 2500);
  }

  function closePurchase() {
    overlay?.querySelector('.alien-shop-purchase')?.remove();
    selectedGood = null;
    overlay?.querySelector('[data-shop-sku]')?.focus();
  }

  function openPurchase(good) {
    if (!overlay) return;
    selectedGood = good;
    const current = balances();
    const enough = Math.max(0, Number(current[good.currency]) || 0) >= good.price;
    const currency = CURRENCIES[good.currency];
    const purchase = document.createElement('div');
    purchase.className = 'alien-shop-purchase';
    purchase.innerHTML = `<section class="alien-shop-purchase-card" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="alien-shop-purchase-title">
      <button type="button" class="alien-shop-purchase-close" data-shop-cancel aria-label="구매 확인 닫기">×</button>
      <small>TRADE AGREEMENT</small><h3 id="alien-shop-purchase-title">교역 확인</h3>
      <div class="alien-shop-purchase-item"><img src="${good.image}" alt="" /><div><strong>${good.name} ${good.rewardType ? '' : good.amount > 1 ? `× ${good.amount}` : ''}</strong><span>${good.detail}</span></div></div>
      ${good.rewardType ? `<div class="alien-shop-exchange-result">교환 후 토륨 <b>+${format(good.rewardAmount)}</b></div>` : ''}
      <div class="alien-shop-purchase-ledger"><span>필요한 ${currency.name}</span>${currencyMarkup(good.currency, good.price)}<span>현재 보유</span>${currencyMarkup(good.currency, current[good.currency])}</div>
      <div class="alien-shop-purchase-actions"><button type="button" data-shop-cancel>취소</button><button type="button" class="alien-shop-confirm" data-shop-confirm ${enough ? '' : 'disabled'}>${enough ? '교역하기' : `${currency.name} 부족`}</button></div>
    </section>`;
    purchase.addEventListener('click', event => {
      if (event.target === purchase || event.target.closest('[data-shop-cancel]')) closePurchase();
      else if (event.target.closest('[data-shop-confirm]')) completePurchase();
    });
    overlay.append(purchase);
    purchase.querySelector('.alien-shop-purchase-close').focus();
  }

  function completePurchase() {
    const good = selectedGood;
    if (!good || !overlay) return;
    if(good.arenaTicket){
      const button=overlay.querySelector('[data-shop-confirm]');if(button?.disabled)return;if(button)button.disabled=true;
      window.purchaseArenaTicket().then(result=>{closePurchase();render();showToast(result.message);if(result.ok)playPurchaseThankyou();}).catch(()=>{if(button)button.disabled=false;showToast('티켓 구매를 저장하지 못했습니다.');});
      return;
    }
    const current = balances();
    const isFeaturedOffer = FEATURED_GOODS.some(offer => offer.sku === good.sku);
    const featuredState = isFeaturedOffer ? dailyFeaturedState() : null;
    if (featuredState?.bought.includes(good.sku)) {
      closePurchase();
      render();
      showToast('오늘 특가 상품은 이미 구매했습니다.');
      return;
    }
    if (Math.max(0, Number(current[good.currency]) || 0) < good.price) {
      closePurchase();
      showToast(`${CURRENCIES[good.currency].name}가 부족합니다.`);
      return;
    }
    if (good.rewardType === 'thorium') {
      current.lionToken -= good.price;
      current.thorium = (Number(current.thorium) || 0) + good.rewardAmount;
      try { localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(current)); }
      catch {
        current.lionToken += good.price;
        current.thorium -= good.rewardAmount;
        closePurchase();
        showToast('교환을 저장하지 못했습니다. 다시 시도해 주세요.');
        return;
      }
      window.updateGameResourceHud?.();
      window.dispatchEvent(new Event('game-resources-updated'));
      playPurchaseThankyou();
      closePurchase();
      render();
      showToast(`토륨 ${format(good.rewardAmount)}개를 교환했습니다.`);
      return;
    }
    if (good.rewardType === 'resource') current[good.itemId] = (Number(current[good.itemId]) || 0) + good.amount;
    else if (!window.grantExplorationLoot?.(good.itemId, good.amount)) {
      closePurchase();
      showToast('교역을 완료하지 못했습니다. 다시 시도해 주세요.');
      return;
    }
    current[good.currency] -= good.price;
    let previousResourceSave = null, previousFeaturedSave = null;
    try {
      previousResourceSave = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
      if (featuredState) previousFeaturedSave = localStorage.getItem(FEATURED_KEY);
    } catch {}
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(current));
      if (featuredState) {
        featuredState.bought.push(good.sku);
        localStorage.setItem(FEATURED_KEY, JSON.stringify(featuredState));
      }
    } catch {
      current[good.currency] += good.price;
      if (good.rewardType === 'resource') current[good.itemId] -= good.amount;
      else window.consumeExplorationLootBatch?.([{ itemId: good.itemId, amount: good.amount }]);
      if (featuredState) featuredState.bought = featuredState.bought.filter(sku => sku !== good.sku);
      try {
        if (previousResourceSave === null) localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEY);
        else localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, previousResourceSave);
        if (featuredState) {
          if (previousFeaturedSave === null) localStorage.removeItem(FEATURED_KEY);
          else localStorage.setItem(FEATURED_KEY, previousFeaturedSave);
        }
      } catch {}
      closePurchase();
      showToast('교역을 저장하지 못했습니다. 다시 시도해 주세요.');
      return;
    }
    window.updateGameResourceHud?.();
    window.dispatchEvent(new Event('game-resources-updated'));
    playPurchaseThankyou();
    closePurchase();
    render();
    showToast(good.rewardType === 'resource' ? `${good.name} ${format(good.amount)}개를 구매했습니다.` : `${good.name} ${good.amount > 1 ? `${good.amount}개` : '1개'}가 물자창고에 보관되었습니다.`);
  }

  function openCashPack(amount) {
    const price = amount * 1500;
    const purchase = document.createElement('div');
    purchase.className = 'alien-shop-purchase';
    purchase.innerHTML = `<section class="alien-shop-purchase-card" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="alien-shop-purchase-title">
      <button type="button" class="alien-shop-purchase-close" data-shop-cancel aria-label="상품 정보 닫기">×</button>
      <small>BLACK MARKET · CASH ITEM</small><h3 id="alien-shop-purchase-title">상품 정보</h3>
      <div class="alien-shop-purchase-item"><img src="icons/lion-token.png" alt="" /><div><strong>사자증표 × ${format(amount)}</strong><span>개당 ₩1,500 · 총 결제 금액 ₩${format(price)}</span></div></div>
      <p class="alien-shop-payment-note">현재 원화 결제 시스템이 연결되지 않아 구매는 아직 진행할 수 없습니다.</p>
      <div class="alien-shop-purchase-actions"><button type="button" data-shop-cancel>닫기</button><button type="button" class="alien-shop-confirm" disabled>결제 연결 준비 중</button></div>
    </section>`;
    purchase.addEventListener('click', event => {
      if (event.target === purchase || event.target.closest('[data-shop-cancel]')) closePurchase();
    });
    overlay.append(purchase);
    purchase.querySelector('.alien-shop-purchase-close').focus();
  }

  function closeShop() {
    if (!overlay) return;
    clearTimeout(toastTimer);
    overlay.remove();
    overlay = null;
    selectedGood = null;
    document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    lastFocus?.focus?.();
  }

  function onKeyDown(event) {
    if (!overlay) return;
    if (event.key === 'Escape') {
      if (overlay.querySelector('.alien-shop-purchase')) closePurchase();
      else closeShop();
      return;
    }
    if (event.key !== 'Tab') return;
    const dialog = overlay.querySelector('.alien-shop-purchase-card') || overlay.querySelector('.alien-shop-dialog');
    const controls = [...dialog.querySelectorAll('button:not([disabled])')];
    if (!controls.length) return;
    const first = controls[0], last = controls[controls.length - 1];
    if (event.shiftKey && (document.activeElement === first || !dialog.contains(document.activeElement))) {
      event.preventDefault();
      last.focus();
    } else if (!event.shiftKey && (document.activeElement === last || !dialog.contains(document.activeElement))) {
      event.preventDefault();
      first.focus();
    }
  }

  window.openAlienShop = (initialTab = 'featured') => {
    const requestedTab = TABS.some(tab => tab.id === initialTab) ? initialTab : 'featured';
    if (overlay) {
      activeTab = requestedTab;
      render();
      overlay.querySelector('.alien-shop-close')?.focus();
      return;
    }
    lastFocus = document.activeElement;
    activeTab = requestedTab;
    try { unlockedTokenPairs = Math.max(0, Math.min(3, Number(localStorage.getItem(STALL_KEY)) || 0)); } catch { unlockedTokenPairs = 0; }
    overlay = document.createElement('div');
    overlay.className = 'alien-shop-overlay';
    overlay.innerHTML = `<section class="alien-shop-dialog" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="alien-shop-title">
      <div class="alien-shop-toolbar"><span class="alien-shop-brand">✦ OUTER EXCHANGE</span><div class="alien-shop-balances"><span title="골드"><img src="gold-resource-icon.svg" alt="골드"/><b data-shop-balance="gold">0</b></span><span title="토륨"><img src="thorium-crystal-icon.png" alt="토륨"/><b data-shop-balance="thorium">0</b></span><span title="사자증표"><img src="icons/lion-token.png" alt="사자증표"/><b data-shop-balance="lionToken">0</b></span></div><button type="button" class="alien-shop-close" aria-label="상점 닫기">×</button></div>
      <div class="alien-shop-hero"><div class="alien-shop-stars" aria-hidden="true"></div><div class="alien-shop-hero-copy"><span class="alien-shop-kicker">은하 변두리 교역소</span><h2 id="alien-shop-title">이방의 상점</h2><p>"먼 성계에서 건너온 물자입니다. 당신의 함대에는 꽤 쓸모가 있겠군요."</p><span class="alien-shop-merchant-sign">✧ 외계 상인 · 아르카</span></div><img class="alien-shop-merchant" src="alien-merchant.png" alt="희귀 물자를 내보이는 외계 상인" /></div>
      <nav class="alien-shop-tabs" role="tablist" aria-label="상점 상품 분류">${TABS.map(tab => `<button type="button" role="tab" data-shop-tab="${tab.id}" aria-selected="false">${tab.label}</button>`).join('')}</nav>
      <div class="alien-shop-section-heading"><div><small>CURATED CARGO</small><strong class="alien-shop-section-title">오늘 특가!</strong></div><span class="alien-shop-section-count"></span></div>
      <div class="alien-shop-grid" role="tabpanel"></div>
      <footer class="alien-shop-footer"><span>구매한 물자는 물자창고에 보관됩니다.</span><button type="button" class="alien-shop-inventory">물자창고 보기 ›</button></footer>
      <div class="alien-shop-toast" role="status" aria-live="polite"></div>
    </section>`;
    overlay.addEventListener('click', event => {
      if (event.target === overlay || event.target.closest('.alien-shop-close')) closeShop();
      else if (event.target.closest('[data-shop-tab]')) {
        activeTab = event.target.closest('[data-shop-tab]').dataset.shopTab;
        render();
      } else if (event.target.closest('[data-shop-sku]')) {
        const good = GOODS.find(entry => entry.sku === event.target.closest('[data-shop-sku]').dataset.shopSku);
        const featuredGood = FEATURED_GOODS.find(entry => entry.sku === event.target.closest('[data-shop-sku]').dataset.shopSku);
        const offer = THORIUM_OFFERS.find(entry => entry.sku === event.target.closest('[data-shop-sku]').dataset.shopSku);
        if (good || featuredGood || offer) openPurchase(good || featuredGood || offer);
      } else if (event.target.closest('[data-cash-pack]')) {
        openCashPack(Number(event.target.closest('[data-cash-pack]').dataset.cashPack));
      } else if (event.target.closest('[data-daily-gift]')) {
        claimDailyGift();
      } else if (event.target.closest('[data-token-unlock]')) {
        unlockTokenPair(Number(event.target.closest('[data-token-unlock]').dataset.tokenUnlock));
      } else if (event.target.closest('.alien-shop-inventory')) {
        closeShop();
        window.openInventory?.();
      }
    });
    document.body.append(overlay);
    document.addEventListener('keydown', onKeyDown);
    render();
    overlay.querySelector('.alien-shop-close').focus();
  };
})();

/* ===== FILE: arena-core.js ===== */
(function(root,factory){const api=factory();if(typeof module==='object'&&module.exports)module.exports=api;else root.ArenaCore=api;})(typeof window==='object'?window:globalThis,()=>{
  'use strict';
  const DAY=86400000,OFFSET=9*3600000;
  const GROUPS=[['Iron',3,'#8c9fa8'],['Bronze',3,'#cd8b55'],['Silver',3,'#c3d5e5'],['Gold',5,'#f7cb59'],['Diamond',5,'#63cfff'],['Platinum',5,'#d4bfff'],['HERO',5,'#ef80ef'],['LEGEND',1,'#ffcf82']];
  const TIERS=[];
  GROUPS.forEach(([group,count,color])=>{for(let division=count;division>0;division--)TIERS.push({group,division,color,label:group==='LEGEND'?group:`${group} ${division}`,index:TIERS.length,min:TIERS.length<24?TIERS.length*200:group==='LEGEND'?6000:4800,cap:group==='HERO'?division:group==='LEGEND'?1:null});});
  const day=now=>Math.floor((now+OFFSET)/DAY);
  const isWednesday=d=>new Date(d*DAY).getUTCDay()===3;
  const clone=o=>JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(o));
  const sorted=players=>[...players].sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score||String(a.id).localeCompare(String(b.id)));
  function standings(players){
    const rows=sorted(players);let heroSeat=0;
    return rows.map((p,i)=>{
      let index=Math.min(23,Math.floor(Math.max(0,p.score)/200));
      if(i===0&&p.score>=6000){index=29;}
      else if(p.score>=4800&&heroSeat<15){heroSeat++;index=heroSeat<=1?28:heroSeat<=3?27:heroSeat<=6?26:heroSeat<=10?25:24;}
      return {...p,rank:i+1,tier:TIERS[index]};
    });
  }
  function rewards(index,weekly=false){
    if(index===29)return weekly?{gold:200000,thorium:400,lionToken:3}:{gold:40000,thorium:80,lionToken:0};
    return weekly?{gold:10000+index*5000,thorium:10+index*10,lionToken:index>=24?2:index>=19?1:0}:{gold:2000+index*1000,thorium:2+index*2,lionToken:0};
  }
  function delta(score,enemy,win){if(win===null)return 0;const expected=1/(1+10**((enemy-score)/400));return win?Math.max(15,Math.min(45,Math.round(60*(1-expected)))):-Math.max(10,Math.min(30,Math.round(40*expected)));}
  function random(seed){let n=seed>>>0;return()=>{n+=0x6D2B79F5;let t=n;t=Math.imul(t^t>>>15,t|1);t^=t+Math.imul(t^t>>>7,t|61);return((t^t>>>14)>>>0)/4294967296;};}
  function chooseOpponent(players,selfId,rng=Math.random){
    const rows=standings(players),me=rows.find(p=>p.id===selfId),others=rows.filter(p=>p.id!==selfId&&p.deck?.slice(0,3).every(Boolean));
    if(!me||!others.length)return null;
    let pool=others.filter(p=>p.tier.label===me.tier.label);
    if(!pool.length)pool=others.filter(p=>p.tier.group===me.tier.group);
    if(!pool.length){const distance=Math.min(...others.map(p=>Math.abs(p.score-me.score)));pool=others.filter(p=>Math.abs(p.score-me.score)<=distance+100);}
    return pool[Math.floor(rng()*pool.length)];
  }
  const level=(tree,key)=>Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(tree?.[key])||0));
  const total=(tree,keys)=>keys.reduce((n,key)=>n+level(tree,key),0);
  function weaponFields(ship,attackType='normal'){
    if(ship?.type==='frigate')return ['frigate'];
    const explicit={cannon:'cannon',missile:'missile',laser:'laser',electric:'electric',piercing:'piercing',fighter:'carrier',carrier:'carrier'}[attackType];
    if(explicit)return [explicit];
    const weapon=String(ship?.profile?.[1]||'');
    const fields=weapon.split('·').map(part=>part.includes('관통')?'piercing':part.includes('함재기')?'carrier':part.includes('미사일')?'missile':part.includes('전기')?'electric':part.includes('레이저')?'laser':part.includes('함포')?'cannon':null).filter(Boolean);
    return [...new Set(fields.length?fields:['cannon'])];
  }
  function weaponBonus(ship,research={},attackType='normal'){
    const fields=weaponFields(ship,attackType);
    const bonuses=fields.map(field=>{
      const tree=research[field+'Tree']||{};
      if(field==='cannon')return {
        damage:(Number(research.cannonBonuses?.damage) || (2*level(tree,'firepower1')+total(tree,['shell1','shell2','shell3']))/10)/100,
        speed:(Number(research.cannonBonuses?.attackSpeed) || (2*level(tree,'firepower1')+total(tree,['reload1','rapidFire','reload2']))/10)/100
      };
      if(field==='carrier')return {damage:(level(tree,'firepower1')+level(tree,'carrierImprovement2'))*.002+total(tree,['weapon1','weapon2','weapon3','newWeapon1','newWeapon2','newWeapon3'])*.001,speed:0};
      if(field==='frigate')return {damage:0,speed:0};
      const roots=total(tree,['firepower1','firepower2','highEfficiency1','highEfficiency2'])*.002;
      const left=field==='missile'?['turret','vls','universalLauncher']:field==='electric'?['powerSupply1','powerSupply2','powerSupply3']:['converter','focusingDevice','controlDevice'];
      const right=field==='missile'?['warhead1','warhead2','warhead3']:field==='electric'?['coil1','coil2','coil3']:['outputBoost1','outputBoost2','outputBoost3'];
      return {damage:roots+total(tree,right)*.001,speed:roots+total(tree,left)*.001};
    });
    return {damage:bonuses.reduce((sum,bonus)=>sum+bonus.damage,0)/bonuses.length,speed:bonuses.reduce((sum,bonus)=>sum+bonus.speed,0)/bonuses.length};
  }
  function frigateBonus(ship,research={}){
    const tree=research.frigateTree||{};
    const key={'건설드론':'constructionDrone','과학 드론':'scienceDrone','과학드론':'scienceDrone','방패선':'shieldShip','리틀보이':'littleBoy','포작선':'bombardShip','레판토':'lepanto'}[ship?.name];
    const rate=(key==='littleBoy'||key==='bombardShip')?.01:.002;
    const tiers=key?[key,key+'2',key+'3'].reduce((sum,node)=>sum+level(tree,node),0):0;
    return {power:key?1+tiers*rate:1,cooldown:Math.max(0,level(tree,'firepower2')*.2+level(tree,'frigateCharging2')*.3),timeReduction:Math.min(.9,level(tree,'frigateExpedition')*.01),durationBonus:Math.max(0,level(tree,'frigatePersistence')*.01),tree};
  }
  function development(ship,research={}){
    const weapon=weaponBonus(ship,research),carrier=research.carrierTree||{},defense=research.defenseTree||{};
    const fields=weaponFields(ship),levels=[...fields,ship?.type==='frigate'?'frigate':null,'defense'].filter(Boolean).reduce((sum,field)=>sum+Object.values(research[field+'Tree']||{}).reduce((n,value)=>n+Math.max(0,Number(value)||0),0),0);
    const mergedMaterial=level(defense,'materialDevelopment');
    return {damage:weapon.damage,speed:weapon.speed,hull:0,carrierHull:(level(carrier,'firepower1')+level(carrier,'carrierImprovement2'))*.002+total(carrier,['hull1','hull2','hull3','hull4','hull5','hull6'])*.001,carrierFighters:level(carrier,'firepower2')>=5?1:0,levels,shield:(total(defense,Array.from({length:6},(_,i)=>'durability'+(i+1)))+total(defense,Array.from({length:5},(_,i)=>'materialDurability'+(i+1)))+mergedMaterial)*.001,shieldDuration:(total(defense,Array.from({length:6},(_,i)=>'duration'+(i+1)))+total(defense,Array.from({length:5},(_,i)=>'materialDuration'+(i+1))))*.001,reduction:(total(defense,Array.from({length:6},(_,i)=>'reduction'+(i+1)))+total(defense,Array.from({length:5},(_,i)=>'materialReinforcement'+(i+1)))+mergedMaterial)*.001,frigate:clone(research.frigateTree||{})};
  }
  function stats(ship,base,research){
    const dev=development(ship,research),stars=ship.enhancementStars??ship.stars??0;
    return {...clone(ship),enhancementStars:stars,hp:Math.max(1,Math.round(base.hp||1000)),dps:Math.max(0,base.dps||0)*(1+dev.damage),skillStars:base.skillStars||stars,factor:base.factor||1,dev};
  }
  function simulate(attack,defense,seed){
    const rng=random(seed),teams=[attack,defense].map((deck,side)=>deck.slice(0,3).map((s,slot)=>({...clone(s),side,slot,maxHp:s.hp,hp:s.hp,shield:0,shieldUntil:0,fighterGuard:Math.max(0,300*(s.name==='우주항모'?6:s.name==='니미츠'?5:s.name==='콜로서스'?4:s.name==='배스천'?3:s.name==='해머'?2:0)*(Number(s.dev?.carrierHull)||0)),stun:0,energy:0,next:slot*.16,core:7+slot,nextSupport:10}))),events=[],frames=[];
    const frigates=[attack[3],defense[3]],frigateResearch=frigates.map(f=>f?frigateBonus(f,{frigateTree:f.dev.frigate}):{timeReduction:0}),supportAt=frigateResearch.map(bonus=>10*(1-bonus.timeReduction));
    const alive=side=>teams[side].filter(u=>u.hp>0);
    const target=side=>{const a=alive(side);return a.find(u=>u.slot===0)&&rng()<.7?a.find(u=>u.slot===0):a[Math.floor(rng()*a.length)];};
    const hurt=(u,n)=>{if(!u)return;const shieldFactor=1-Math.max(0,Math.min(.5,Number(u.dev?.reduction)||0)),absorbed=Math.min(n,u.shield/shieldFactor);u.shield=Math.max(0,u.shield-absorbed*shieldFactor);const escortAbsorbed=Math.min(n-absorbed,u.fighterGuard);u.fighterGuard-=escortAbsorbed;u.hp=Math.max(0,u.hp-(n-absorbed-escortAbsorbed));};
    let time=0,nextFrame=0;
    for(;time<=120&&alive(0).length&&alive(1).length;time+=.05){
      const pending=[];
      teams.flat().forEach(u=>{
        if(u.hp<=0)return;if(u.shieldUntil<=time)u.shield=0;if(u.stun>time){u.next+=.05;u.core+=.05;return;}
        if(time>=u.next){u.next=time+1/(1+(Number(u.dev?.speed)||0));const enemy=target(1-u.side);if(enemy)pending.push(()=>{hurt(enemy,u.dps*(.9+rng()*.2));events.push({time,side:u.side,slot:u.slot,to:enemy.slot,type:'shot',weapon:u.profile?.[1]});});}
        u.energy=Math.min(100,100*(1-(u.core-time)/10));
        if(time>=u.core){u.core=time+10;u.energy=0;const enemy=target(1-u.side),skill=u.profile?.[4]||'';if(!enemy)return;
          pending.push(()=>{
            if(/수리|수복/.test(skill)){const friend=alive(u.side).sort((a,b)=>a.hp/a.maxHp-b.hp/b.maxHp)[0];if(friend)friend.hp=Math.min(friend.maxHp,friend.hp+friend.maxHp*.18*u.factor);}
            else if(/보호|방패|석화/.test(skill))alive(u.side).forEach(friend=>{friend.shield=friend.maxHp*.15*(1+u.dev.shield);friend.shieldUntil=time+5*(1+u.dev.shieldDuration);});
            else if(/그물/.test(skill)){enemy.stun=time+3;hurt(enemy,u.dps*2);}
            else {const multi=/광역|난사|초신성|번개|포화|다련장/.test(skill);(multi?alive(1-u.side):[enemy]).forEach(v=>hurt(v,u.dps*(multi?1.8:4)*(u.skillStars>=5?1.2:1)));if(/기절/.test(skill))enemy.stun=time+1.5;}
            events.push({time,side:u.side,slot:u.slot,to:enemy.slot,type:'core',text:skill.split(' · ')[0]});
          });
        }
      });
      [0,1].forEach(side=>{const f=frigates[side];if(!f||time<supportAt[side]||!alive(side).length)return;const frigate=frigateBonus(f,{frigateTree:f.dev.frigate});supportAt[side]=time+Math.max(.1,(20-frigate.cooldown)*(1-frigate.timeReduction));pending.push(()=>{
        const friends=alive(side),foes=alive(1-side),boost=f.factor*frigate.power;
        if(/건설|과학/.test(f.name))friends.forEach(u=>u.hp=Math.min(u.maxHp,u.hp+u.maxHp*.08*boost));
        else if(/방패/.test(f.name))friends.forEach(u=>{u.shield=u.maxHp*.25*f.factor*(1+u.dev.shield);u.shieldUntil=time+5*frigate.power*(1+u.dev.shieldDuration)*(1+frigate.durationBonus);});
        else foes.forEach(u=>hurt(u,(f.name==='리틀보이'?1000:f.name==='포작선'?500:350)*boost));
        events.push({time,side,slot:3,to:0,type:'support',text:f.name});
      });});
      pending.forEach(fn=>fn());
      if(time+.001>=nextFrame){frames.push({time,teams:teams.map(team=>team.map(u=>({hp:u.hp,maxHp:u.maxHp,shield:u.shield,energy:u.energy,stunned:u.stun>time})))});nextFrame+=.5;}
    }
    const ratio=teams.map(team=>team.reduce((n,u)=>n+u.hp/u.maxHp,0));
    const win=ratio[0]===ratio[1]?null:ratio[0]>ratio[1];
    frames.push({time,teams:teams.map(team=>team.map(u=>({hp:u.hp,maxHp:u.maxHp,shield:u.shield,energy:u.energy})))});
    return {win,time,frames,events,seed};
  }
  return {TIERS,GROUPS,DAY,OFFSET,day,isWednesday,standings,rewards,delta,random,chooseOpponent,weaponFields,weaponBonus,frigateBonus,development,stats,simulate,clone};
});

/* ===== FILE: arena.js ===== */
(() => {
  'use strict';
  const C=window.ArenaCore,KEY='starfleet-async-arena-v1',WAL=KEY+'-transaction',RESOURCE='starfleet-game-resources-v1',SELF='player';
  const roles=['선봉','좌익','우익','프리깃'],fmt=n=>Math.round(n||0).toLocaleString('ko-KR'),esc=s=>String(s??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
  let overlay=null,lastFocus=null,playTimer=0,playing=false,draft=null,busy=false;
  const read=(key,fallback)=>{const raw=localStorage.getItem(key);return raw?JSON.parse(raw):fallback;};
  function recover(){const tx=read(WAL,null);if(tx){localStorage.setItem(KEY,JSON.stringify(tx.state));localStorage.setItem(RESOURCE,JSON.stringify(tx.resources));localStorage.removeItem(WAL);if(window.gameResources)Object.assign(window.gameResources,tx.resources);}}
  function commit(state,resources){
    if(resources){localStorage.setItem(WAL,JSON.stringify({state,resources}));try{recover();}catch{/* Durable journal will replay on the next operation. */}Object.assign(window.gameResources||(window.gameResources={}),resources);}
    else localStorage.setItem(KEY,JSON.stringify(state));
    if(resources){window.updateGameResourceHud?.();window.dispatchEvent(new Event('game-resources-updated'));}
  }
  const lock=fn=>navigator.locks?navigator.locks.request('starfleet-arena',fn):Promise.resolve().then(fn);
  const research=()=>read('starfleet-tactical-weapon-research-v1',{});
  const ident=s=>s?{name:s.name,type:s.type,rarity:s.rarity,enhancementStars:Number(s.enhancementStars??s.stars)||0}:null;
  const variantKey=s=>s?`${s.type}|${s.name}|${s.rarity}|${s.enhancementStars??s.stars??0}`:'';
  const shipKey=s=>s?`${s.type}|${s.name}`:'';
  const owned=()=> (window.getOwnedFleetVariants?.()||[]).filter(s=>!s.isFrame).map(s=>({...s,enhancementStars:Number(s.stars??s.enhancementStars)||0}));
  const resolve=deck=>deck.map(ref=>owned().find(s=>variantKey(s)===variantKey(ref))||null);
  const snapshot=(ships,dev=research())=>ships.map(s=>s?C.stats(s,window.getArenaShipBaseStats(s),dev):null);
  const complete=deck=>deck?.length===4&&deck.slice(0,3).every(s=>s&&s.type!=='frigate')&&(!deck[3]||deck[3].type==='frigate')&&new Set(deck.filter(Boolean).map(shipKey)).size===deck.filter(Boolean).length;
  const frigateUnlocked=()=>Math.max(research().frigate?.level||0,research().frigateTree?.firepower1||0)>=1;
  function makeBots(){
    const catalog=(window.getFleetCatalog?.()||[]).filter(s=>!s.isFrame),main=catalog.filter(s=>s.type!=='frigate'&&s.profile?.[0]),support=catalog.filter(s=>s.type==='frigate');
    if(main.length<3)throw Error('함선 정보를 불러오는 중입니다. 잠시 후 다시 열어주세요.');
    const names=['NOVA PRIME','청룡 함대','SIRIUS','ORION','RED COMET','은하수','POLARIS','아틀라스','VANGUARD','해오라기','ACE-77','STARBLADE','SOLARIS','NEBULA','이클립스','묵룡'];
    const scores=[6400,...Array.from({length:15},(_,i)=>5950-i*65),...Array.from({length:24},(_,i)=>i*200+70),0,45,110,170];
    return scores.map((score,i)=>{const rng=C.random(801+i),pool=main.filter(s=>score>=1800||s.rarity==='R'||s.rarity==='SR'),choices=[...pool],stars=Math.min(5,Math.floor(score/1000));
      const ships=Array.from({length:3},()=>{const index=Math.floor(rng()*choices.length);return {...choices.splice(index,1)[0],enhancementStars:stars};});
      ships.push(support.length?{...support[i%support.length],enhancementStars:Math.min(3,stars)}:null);
      const lv=Math.min(5,Math.floor(score/1000)),dev={};['cannon','missile','laser','piercing','electric','carrier','frigate'].forEach(k=>dev[k+'Tree']={firepower1:lv,highEfficiency1:lv});dev.cannonBonuses={damage:lv*.2,attackSpeed:lv*.2};
      return {id:'bot-'+i,name:names[i%names.length]+(i>=16?' '+(Math.floor(i/16)+1):''),score,bot:true,tokens:12+Math.floor(score/35)+(i*13)%23,deck:snapshot(ships,dev)};
    });
  }
  function initial(){const deck=(window.getFleetAssignments?.()||[]);while(deck.length<4)deck.push(null);return {version:1,score:0,wins:0,losses:0,draws:0,tickets:0,freeUsed:0,freeDay:C.day(Date.now()),settledDay:C.day(Date.now()),attack:deck.map(ident),defense:deck.map(ident),defenseSnapshot:complete(deck)?snapshot(deck):null,bots:makeBots(),inbox:[],history:[],pending:null};}
  const playerName=()=>{const p=read('starfleet-commander-profile-v1',{});return p.lastName?`${p.character||''} ${p.lastName}`:'나의 함대';};
  const board=state=>C.standings([{id:SELF,name:playerName(),score:state.score,deck:state.defenseSnapshot},...state.bots]);
  const mine=state=>board(state).find(p=>p.id===SELF);
  function settle(state,now=Date.now()){
    const today=C.day(now);if(today>state.freeDay){state.freeDay=today;state.freeUsed=0;}
    if(today>state.settledDay){
      if(state.wins+state.losses+state.draws>0){const tier=mine(state).tier;let weeks=0;for(let d=state.settledDay+1;d<=today;d++)if(C.isWednesday(d))weeks++;
        const days=today-state.settledDay,daily=C.rewards(tier.index),weekly=C.rewards(tier.index,true),amounts={};['gold','thorium','lionToken'].forEach(k=>amounts[k]=daily[k]*days+weekly[k]*weeks);
        state.inbox.push({id:`settle-${state.settledDay}-${today}`,label:`${tier.label} · 일일 ${days}회${weeks?' / 주간 '+weeks+'회':''}`,amounts});
      }
      state.settledDay=today;
    }
    return state;
  }
  function state(){recover();let s=read(KEY,null);if(!s)s=initial();const before=JSON.stringify(s);settle(s);const defense=resolve(s.defense||[]);if(complete(defense))s.defenseSnapshot=snapshot(defense);if(before!==JSON.stringify(s)||!localStorage.getItem(KEY))commit(s);return s;}
  function note(text){const n=overlay?.querySelector('[data-arena-note]');if(n)n.textContent=text;else window.notice?.(text);}
  async function act(fn){if(busy)return;busy=true;try{await lock(fn);}catch(e){note(e.message||'저장할 수 없습니다. 다시 시도해주세요.');}finally{busy=false;}}
  function emblem(tier,small=false){
    const symbols={Iron:'M22 36l14 8 14-8',Bronze:'M20 29l16 9 16-9M20 39l16 9 16-9',Silver:'M20 25l16 9 16-9M20 35l16 9 16-9M20 45l16 9 16-9',Gold:'M18 27l18 10 18-10M18 36l18 10 18-10M18 45l18 10 18-10',Diamond:'M17 29l8-10h22l8 10-19 28-19-28h38M25 19l11 38 11-38',Platinum:'M36 22v31M34 32L17 23l7 21 12 9 12-9 7-21-17 9M27 22l9-6 9 6',HERO:'M36 16l6 14 17 1-13 11 4 16-14-9-14 9 4-16-13-11 17-1Z',LEGEND:'M17 26l10 8 9-18 9 18 10-8-5 25H22Z M23 56h26'};
    return `<svg class="arena-emblem${small?' small':''}" viewBox="0 0 72 80" style="--rank-color:${tier.color}" aria-label="${tier.label}"><path class="crest" d="M36 3 66 19v39L36 77 6 58V19Z"/><path class="inset" d="M36 9 60 23v32L36 70 12 55V23Z"/><path class="sigil" d="${symbols[tier.group]}"/></svg>`;
  }
  const shipCard=(s,slot,click='')=>`<button type="button" class="arena-ship" ${click} ${!click?'disabled':''}><small>${roles[slot]}</small>${s?`<img src="${esc(s.image)}" alt="${esc(s.name)}"/><b>${esc(s.name)}</b><em>${s.rarity} · ${s.enhancementStars??s.stars??0}성</em>`:'<span class="arena-empty">＋</span><b>선택</b>'}</button>`;
  function deckBlock(kind,label,s){const deck=resolve(s[kind]);return `<section class="arena-deck arena-deck-${kind}"><header><div><small>${kind==='defense'?'DEFENSE FLEET · GUARD':'ATTACK FLEET · ASSAULT'}</small><h3>${label}</h3></div><button data-edit-deck="${kind}">등록 / 변경</button></header><div class="arena-ships">${deck.map((ship,i)=>shipCard(ship,i)).join('')}</div>${kind==='defense'?'<p>저장된 방어 함대는 상대의 공격에 자동으로 대응합니다.</p>':''}</section>`;}
  function shell(title,content){const isHome=title==='ARENA',isCombat=title==='함대 교전';overlay.innerHTML=`<article class="arena-panel${isHome?' arena-home':''}${isCombat?' arena-combat-panel':''}" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="${esc(isHome?'아레나':title)}"><header class="arena-header">${isHome?'':`<div><small>UNITED STAR FLEET · ASYNC ARENA</small><h2>${title}</h2></div>`}<button data-arena-close aria-label="닫기">×</button></header>${content}<p class="arena-note" data-arena-note role="status"></p></article>`;overlay.querySelector('[data-arena-close]').onclick=()=>playing?note('자동전투가 진행 중입니다.'):draft?(draft=null,render()):close();}
  function render(){
    const s=state(),rows=board(s),me=mine(s),top=rows.slice(0,3),ticketLeft=Math.max(0,5-s.freeUsed),daily=C.rewards(me.tier.index),weekly=C.rewards(me.tier.index,true);
    shell('ARENA',`
      <div class="arena-season"><img src="icons/arena-button.png" alt="Arena"/><div><small>SEASON 01</small><h3>유틀란트 해전</h3></div></div>
      <section class="arena-ranking">
        <div class="arena-podium">${[1,0,2].map(i=>{const p=top[i];return `<div class="place-${i+1}"><span>${i===0?'♛':i+1}</span>${emblem(p.tier)}<button type="button" class="arena-podium-name" data-player-id="${esc(p.id)}">${esc(p.name)}</button><small>${p.tier.label}</small><strong>${fmt(p.score)}</strong></div>`;}).join('')}</div>
        <div class="arena-rank-list">${rows.slice(3).map(p=>`<div class="${p.id===SELF?'is-self':''}"><strong>${p.rank}</strong>${emblem(p.tier,true)}<span><button type="button" class="arena-player-link" data-player-id="${esc(p.id)}">${esc(p.name)}</button></span><em>${fmt(p.score)}</em></div>`).join('')}</div>
      </section>
      <button class="arena-battle-button" data-match><strong>아레나 출전</strong><span>${ticketLeft?'무료 대전 '+ticketLeft+' / 5':'아레나 티켓 1장 사용'}</span></button>
      ${deckBlock('defense','수비 함대',s)}${deckBlock('attack','공격 함대',s)}
      <footer class="arena-status">${emblem(me.tier)}<div><small>현재 등급</small><div class="arena-tier-line"><b>${me.tier.label}</b><button type="button" class="arena-tier-help" data-rules aria-label="등급과 보상 정보">?</button></div><span>${fmt(s.score)}점 · ${me.rank}위</span></div><div><small>대전 기록</small><b>${s.wins}승 ${s.losses}패</b><span>티켓 ${s.tickets}장</span></div></footer>
      <p class="arena-settlement">매일 00:00 · 수요일 00:00 KST<br>일일 ${rewardText(daily)} / 주간 ${rewardText(weekly)}</p>`);
    overlay.querySelector('[data-match]').onclick=()=>startMatch(false);
    overlay.querySelectorAll('[data-edit-deck]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>editDeck(b.dataset.editDeck));
    overlay.querySelector('[data-rules]').onclick=rules;
    overlay.querySelectorAll('[data-player-id]').forEach(button=>button.onclick=()=>showPlayerProfile(rows.find(p=>p.id===button.dataset.playerId)));
  }
  function showPlayerProfile(player){
    if(!player)return;
    document.querySelector('.arena-player-popup')?.remove();
    const roster=player.id===SELF?owned():((player.deck||[]).filter(Boolean));
    const rarityRank={R:1,SR:2,SSR:3,L:4};
    const best=[...roster].sort((a,b)=>(rarityRank[b.rarity]||0)-(rarityRank[a.rarity]||0)||(Number(b.enhancementStars??b.stars)||0)-(Number(a.enhancementStars??a.stars)||0))[0];
    const levels=player.id===SELF
      ?Math.max(0,...roster.map(ship=>C.development(ship,research()).levels||0))
      :Math.max(0,...roster.map(ship=>Number(ship.dev?.levels)||0));
    const botIndex=Number(String(player.id).replace('bot-',''))||0;
    const tokenCount=player.id===SELF
      ?Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number((window.gameResources||read(RESOURCE,{})).lionToken)||0))
      :Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(player.tokens??(12+Math.floor(player.score/35)+(botIndex*13)%23))||0));
    const popup=document.createElement('div');popup.className='arena-player-popup';popup.innerHTML=`<section class="arena-player-card" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="함장 정보"><header><small>FLEET CAPTAIN</small><button type="button" aria-label="닫기">×</button></header><h3>${esc(player.name)}</h3><dl><div><dt>최고 등급 함선</dt><dd>${best?`${esc(best.name)} · ${esc(best.rarity||'R')} · ${Number(best.enhancementStars??best.stars)||0}성`:'등록 정보 없음'}</dd></div><div><dt>최고 개발 단계</dt><dd>Lv. ${fmt(levels)}</dd></div><div><dt>사자증표</dt><dd>${fmt(tokenCount)}개</dd></div></dl></section>`;
    const close=()=>{popup.remove();document.removeEventListener('keydown',onKey);};
    const onKey=event=>{if(event.key==='Escape'){event.stopPropagation();close();}};
    popup.addEventListener('click',event=>{if(event.target===popup||event.target.closest('button'))close();});
    document.addEventListener('keydown',onKey);document.body.append(popup);popup.querySelector('button').focus();
  }
  const rewardText=r=>`${fmt(r.gold)} 골드 · ${fmt(r.thorium)} 토륨${r.lionToken?' · 증표 '+r.lionToken:''}`;
  function sub(title,html){shell(title,html);overlay.querySelector('[data-arena-close]').onclick=render;}
  function rules(){sub('등급과 보상',`<p class="arena-explanation">같은 등급 우선 무작위 매칭. 동점 상대 기준 승리 +30 / 패배 −20점, 상대 점수에 따라 +15~45 / −10~30점. 무승부 0점, 최저 0점.<br>HERO는 4,800점 이상 순위순 15명, LEGEND는 6,000점 이상 전체 1위 1명입니다. 정원이 찬 HERO 후보는 Platinum 1에 머뭅니다.<br>첫 대전 이후 매일 0시, 수요일 0시에 당시 등급으로 보상함에 지급됩니다. 한국 시간 기준이며 미접속 보상도 다음 접속 시 정산됩니다. 시즌 점수는 자동 초기화하지 않습니다.</p><div class="arena-tier-table"><div><b>등급 / 조건</b><b>일일 보상</b><b>주간 보상</b></div>${C.TIERS.map(t=>`<div><span>${emblem(t,true)}<b>${t.label}</b><small>${t.cap?`${fmt(t.min)}점+ · ${t.cap}석`:`${fmt(t.min)} ~ ${t.index===23?'상위석 미달 시 유지':fmt(t.min+199)}`}</small></span><span>${rewardText(C.rewards(t.index))}</span><span>${rewardText(C.rewards(t.index,true))}</span></div>`).join('')}</div><p class="arena-explanation">보상 가치 기준: 사자증표 1 = 토륨 100 = 골드 100,000.<br>이 리그는 현재 기기에 저장되는 가상 상대 테스트 환경입니다. 실제 이용자 매칭·방어전 수신·서버 시간 정산은 서버 연결 후 제공됩니다.</p>`);if(state().inbox.length){const claim=document.createElement('button');claim.className='arena-save';claim.textContent='도착한 보상 확인';claim.onclick=inbox;overlay.querySelector('.arena-panel').insertBefore(claim,overlay.querySelector('.arena-note'));}}
  function editDeck(kind){draft={kind,slots:C.clone(state()[kind])};paintDeck();}
  function paintDeck(){
    const deck=resolve(draft.slots);shell(draft.kind==='defense'?'방어 덱 편성':'공격 덱 편성',`<p class="arena-explanation">선봉 · 좌익 · 우익에 서로 다른 함선을 선택하세요. 프리깃은 연구 개방 후 선택할 수 있습니다.</p><div class="arena-ships">${deck.map((s,i)=>shipCard(s,i,`data-slot="${i}"`)).join('')}</div><div class="arena-tools"><button data-import>함대관리 편성 불러오기</button><button data-save class="arena-save">편성 저장</button></div><p class="arena-explanation">등급·성급은 보유 변형과 연결됩니다. 방어 덱은 저장 시 개발 수치까지 복사되며, 개발 후 다시 저장하면 갱신됩니다.</p>`);
    overlay.querySelectorAll('[data-slot]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>pick(Number(b.dataset.slot)));
    overlay.querySelector('[data-import]').onclick=()=>{draft.slots=Array.from({length:4},(_,i)=>ident(window.getFleetAssignments?.()[i]));paintDeck();};
    overlay.querySelector('[data-save]').onclick=()=>act(()=>{const deck=resolve(draft.slots);if(!complete(deck))throw Error('선봉·좌익·우익을 모두 서로 다른 보유 함선으로 편성해주세요.');if(deck[3]&&!frigateUnlocked())throw Error('프리깃 연구가 필요합니다.');const s=state();s[draft.kind]=deck.map(ident);if(draft.kind==='defense')s.defenseSnapshot=snapshot(deck);commit(s);draft=null;render();note('편성을 저장했습니다.');});
  }
  function pick(slot){
    if(slot===3&&!frigateUnlocked()){note('연구실에서 프리깃을 먼저 개발해주세요.');return;}
    const list=owned().filter(s=>slot===3?s.type==='frigate':s.type!=='frigate').sort((a,b)=>({L:4,SSR:3,SR:2,R:1}[b.rarity]-{L:4,SSR:3,SR:2,R:1}[a.rarity])||b.enhancementStars-a.enhancementStars);
    shell(roles[slot]+' 선택',`<div class="arena-picker">${list.map((s,i)=>{const used=draft.slots.some((v,k)=>k!==slot&&shipKey(v)===shipKey(s));return `<button data-choice="${i}" ${used?'disabled':''}><img src="${esc(s.image)}" alt=""/><b>${esc(s.name)}</b><span>${s.rarity} · ${s.enhancementStars}성${used?' · 편성됨':''}</span></button>`;}).join('')||'<p>보유한 함선이 없습니다. 함대관리에서 함선을 확보해주세요.</p>'}</div>${slot===3?'<button data-clear-slot>프리깃 해제</button>':''}`);
    overlay.querySelector('[data-arena-close]').onclick=paintDeck;
    overlay.querySelectorAll('[data-choice]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{draft.slots[slot]=ident(list[Number(b.dataset.choice)]);paintDeck();});
    overlay.querySelector('[data-clear-slot]')?.addEventListener('click',()=>{draft.slots[slot]=null;paintDeck();});
  }
  async function startMatch(practice){await act(()=>{
    const s=state();if(s.pending){enterArenaBattle(s.pending);return;}
    const fleet=practice?s.defenseSnapshot:snapshot(resolve(s.attack));
    if(!complete(fleet)||!complete(s.defenseSnapshot))throw Error('공격 덱과 방어 덱을 먼저 등록해주세요.');
    if(!practice&&resolve(s.attack)[3]&&!frigateUnlocked())throw Error('프리깃 연구를 먼저 개방해주세요.');
    if(!practice&&s.freeUsed>=5&&s.tickets<1)throw Error('무료 대전을 모두 사용했습니다. 상점에서 아레나 티켓을 구매해주세요.');
    const seed=crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint32Array(1))[0],enemy=C.chooseOpponent([{id:SELF,score:s.score,deck:s.defenseSnapshot},...s.bots],SELF,C.random(seed));if(!enemy)throw Error('매칭 가능한 상대가 없습니다.');
    const result=C.simulate(fleet,enemy.deck,seed),match={id:crypto.randomUUID(),enemy:{id:enemy.id,name:enemy.name,score:enemy.score,tier:enemy.tier},fleet,enemyDeck:enemy.deck,result,practice,scoreBefore:s.score,created:Date.now()};
    if(!practice){const ticketUsed=s.freeUsed>=5;if(ticketUsed)s.tickets--;else s.freeUsed++;match.ticketUsed=ticketUsed;s.pending=match;commit(s);}enterArenaBattle(match);
  });}
  function enterArenaBattle(match){
    draft=null;playing=false;overlay?.remove();overlay=null;window.battleArenaMatch=match;
    window.notice?.('아레나 입장완료');window.show?.('battle');
    requestAnimationFrame(()=>window.prepareBattle?.({arenaMatch:match}));
  }
  window.cancelArenaBattle=async match=>{
    await lock(()=>{const s=state();if(s.pending?.id!==match?.id)return;s.pending=null;if(match.ticketUsed)s.tickets++;else s.freeUsed=Math.max(0,s.freeUsed-1);commit(s);});
    window.battleArenaMatch=null;window.show?.('home');window.openArena?.();
  };
  window.completeArenaBattle=match=>lock(()=>{
    const s=state(),outcome=match.forfeited?false:match.result.win;
    if(s.pending?.id===match.id){const change=C.delta(match.scoreBefore,match.enemy.score,outcome);s.score=Math.max(0,s.score+change);if(outcome===null)s.draws++;else if(outcome)s.wins++;else s.losses++;const bot=s.bots.find(b=>b.id===match.enemy.id);if(bot)bot.score=Math.max(0,bot.score-change);s.history.unshift({id:match.id,date:Date.now(),enemy:match.enemy.name,win:outcome,delta:change,score:s.score});s.history=s.history.slice(0,40);s.pending=null;commit(s);}
    const me=mine(s),history=s.history.find(item=>item.id===match.id);return {win:outcome,delta:history?.delta||0,score:s.score,rank:me.rank,tier:me.tier.label};
  });
  window.returnFromArenaBattle=()=>{window.battleArenaMatch=null;window.show?.('home');window.openArena?.();};
  function play(match){
    playing=true;draft=null;let index=0;
    shell('함대 교전',`<div class="arena-versus"><div class="arena-side-tag enemy-tag"><small>상대 함대</small><b>${esc(match.enemy.name)}</b><span>${esc(match.enemy.tier?.label||'ARENA')}</span></div><em>VS</em><div class="arena-side-tag friendly-tag"><small>${match.practice?'방어 테스트':'나의 공격 함대'}</small><b>${esc(playerName())}</b><span>${fmt(match.scoreBefore)}점</span></div></div><p class="arena-auto">AI 자동 전투 <i></i> <span data-clock>00:00</span></p><div class="arena-field"><div class="arena-centerline"><span>ENGAGEMENT ZONE</span></div><div class="arena-combat-team enemy">${match.enemyDeck.slice(0,3).map((s,i)=>unit(s,1,i)).join('')}</div><svg class="arena-trails" viewBox="0 0 100 100" preserveAspectRatio="none" aria-hidden="true"></svg><div class="arena-combat-team friendly">${match.fleet.slice(0,3).map((s,i)=>unit(s,0,i)).join('')}</div><div class="arena-field-corner enemy-corner">ENEMY FLEET</div><div class="arena-field-corner friendly-corner">YOUR FLEET</div></div><div class="arena-combat-log" aria-live="polite"></div>`);
    const tick=()=>{const frame=match.result.frames[index++];if(!frame){clearInterval(playTimer);playTimer=0;finish(match);return;}
      overlay.querySelector('[data-clock]').textContent=`${Math.floor(frame.time/60).toString().padStart(2,'0')}:${Math.floor(frame.time%60).toString().padStart(2,'0')}`;
      frame.teams.forEach((team,side)=>team.forEach((u,i)=>{const card=overlay.querySelector(`[data-unit="${side}-${i}"]`);card.classList.toggle('destroyed',u.hp<=0);card.classList.toggle('stunned',!!u.stunned);card.querySelector('meter').value=u.hp/u.maxHp;card.querySelector('small').textContent=`${fmt(u.hp)} / ${fmt(u.maxHp)}`;card.querySelector('.arena-energy').style.width=u.energy+'%';}));
      const effects=match.result.events.filter(e=>e.time>frame.time-.5&&e.time<=frame.time);
      overlay.querySelector('.arena-trails').innerHTML=effects.filter(e=>e.slot<3).map(e=>`<line x1="${[50,18,82][e.slot]}" y1="${e.side?25:75}" x2="${[50,18,82][e.to]}" y2="${e.side?75:25}" stroke="${e.side?'#ff8b83':'#7cf5ff'}" stroke-width="${e.type==='core'?1.2:.35}"/>`).join('');
      const log=effects.filter(e=>e.type!=='shot').map(e=>`${e.side?'상대':'아군'} · ${esc(e.text)}`).join('<br>');if(log)overlay.querySelector('.arena-combat-log').innerHTML=log;
    };tick();playTimer=setInterval(tick,125);
  }
  const unit=(s,side,i)=>`<div class="arena-unit slot-${i}" data-unit="${side}-${i}" aria-label="${side?'상대':'아군'} ${esc(s.name)}"><b>${esc(s.name)}</b><img src="${esc(s.image)}" alt=""/><meter min="0" max="1" value="1"></meter><div class="arena-energy-track"><i class="arena-energy"></i></div><small>${fmt(s.hp)} / ${fmt(s.hp)}</small></div>`;
  async function finish(match){
    try{await lock(()=>{let change=0;const s=state();if(!match.practice&&s.pending?.id===match.id){change=C.delta(match.scoreBefore,match.enemy.score,match.result.win);s.score=Math.max(0,s.score+change);if(match.result.win===null)s.draws++;else if(match.result.win)s.wins++;else s.losses++;const bot=s.bots.find(b=>b.id===match.enemy.id);if(bot)bot.score=Math.max(0,bot.score-change);s.history.unshift({id:match.id,date:Date.now(),enemy:match.enemy.name,win:match.result.win,delta:change,score:s.score});s.history=s.history.slice(0,40);s.pending=null;commit(s);}else if(!match.practice){change=s.history.find(h=>h.id===match.id)?.delta||0;}
      playing=false;const me=mine(s),label=match.result.win===null?'DRAW':match.result.win?'VICTORY':'DEFEAT';
      shell(label,`<div class="arena-result ${match.result.win?'victory':''}">${emblem(me.tier)}<h3>${match.practice?'방어 테스트 종료':me.tier.label}</h3><strong>${match.practice?'점수·횟수 변동 없음':`${change>0?'+':''}${change} POINTS`}</strong><p>${esc(match.enemy.name)} · ${Math.ceil(match.result.time)}초 교전</p><p>${match.practice?'':fmt(s.score)+'점 · '+me.rank+'위'}</p><button class="arena-save" data-result-back>아레나로 돌아가기</button></div>`);overlay.querySelector('[data-result-back]').onclick=render;overlay.querySelector('[data-arena-close]').onclick=render;
    });}catch(e){playing=false;note('결과 저장을 다시 시도해주세요. '+e.message);const b=document.createElement('button');b.textContent='결과 저장 재시도';b.onclick=()=>finish(match);overlay.querySelector('.arena-panel').append(b);}
  }
  function inbox(){const s=state();sub('아레나 보상함',`<div class="arena-inbox">${s.inbox.map(r=>`<div><b>${esc(r.label)}</b><p>${rewardText(r.amounts)}</p></div>`).join('')||'<p>도착한 보상이 없습니다. 첫 대전 후 다음 정산 시각부터 보상이 지급됩니다.</p>'}</div>${s.inbox.length?'<button class="arena-save" data-claim>모두 수령</button>':''}`);overlay.querySelector('[data-claim]')?.addEventListener('click',()=>act(()=>{const next=state(),resources={...(window.gameResources||read(RESOURCE,{}))};if(!next.inbox.length)return;next.inbox.forEach(r=>Object.entries(r.amounts).forEach(([k,v])=>resources[k]=(resources[k]||0)+v));next.inbox=[];commit(next,resources);inbox();note('보상을 수령했습니다.');}));}
  function history(){sub('최근 전투',`<div class="arena-history">${state().history.map(h=>`<div><b>${h.win===null?'무승부':h.win?'승리':'패배'} · ${esc(h.enemy)}</b><span>${h.delta>0?'+':''}${h.delta}점</span><small>${new Date(h.date).toLocaleString('ko-KR')} · ${fmt(h.score)}점</small></div>`).join('')||'<p>아직 대전 기록이 없습니다.</p>'}</div>`);}
  function close(){if(playing)return;clearInterval(playTimer);overlay?.remove();overlay=null;draft=null;lastFocus?.focus();}
  window.openArena=()=>{if(overlay){overlay.focus();return;}lastFocus=document.activeElement;overlay=document.createElement('div');overlay.className='arena-overlay';document.body.append(overlay);overlay.onclick=e=>{if(e.target===overlay&&!playing)close();};overlay.onkeydown=e=>{if(e.key==='Escape'){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();if(!playing){if(draft){draft=null;render();}else close();}}if(e.key==='Tab'){const items=[...overlay.querySelectorAll('button:not(:disabled),input')],first=items[0],last=items[items.length-1];if(e.shiftKey&&document.activeElement===first){e.preventDefault();last?.focus();}else if(!e.shiftKey&&document.activeElement===last){e.preventDefault();first?.focus();}}};try{const s=state();if(s.pending)enterArenaBattle(s.pending);else render();overlay?.querySelector('button')?.focus();}catch(e){shell('ARENA','');note(e.message);}};
  window.getArenaTickets=()=>{try{return state().tickets;}catch{return 0;}};
  window.purchaseArenaTicket=()=>lock(()=>{const s=state(),resources={...(window.gameResources||read(RESOURCE,{}))};if((resources.thorium||0)<50)return {ok:false,message:'토륨이 부족합니다.'};resources.thorium-=50;s.tickets++;commit(s,resources);return {ok:true,message:'아레나 티켓 1장을 구매했습니다.'};});
  window.addEventListener('storage',e=>{if(e.key===KEY&&overlay&&!playing&&!draft)render();});
  setInterval(()=>{if(overlay&&!playing&&!draft&&overlay.querySelector('[data-match]')){try{const saved=read(KEY,null);if(saved&&C.day(Date.now())>saved.freeDay)render();}catch{}}},1000);
  window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>{try{recover();if(localStorage.getItem(KEY))state();}catch{}});
})();

/* ===== FILE: battle-engine.js ===== */
(() => {
  'use strict';

  const RARITY_COLORS = { L: '#ff526d', SSR: '#ffd45b', SR: '#c883ff', R: '#69d8ff' };
  const ENHANCEMENT_RATE = { R: .2, SR: .2, SSR: .3, L: .4 };
  let activeDamageSource = null;
  function withDamageSource(source, action) {
    const previous = activeDamageSource;
    activeDamageSource = source;
    try { return action(); } finally { activeDamageSource = previous; }
  }
  function combatStatFactor(source) {
    const ship = source?.owner?.ship || source?.ship || source;
    if (!ship || !ship.type) return 1;
    const stars = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(ship.enhancementStars ?? ship.stars) || 0));
    const rarity = ship.rarity || 'R';
    return 1 + stars * (ENHANCEMENT_RATE[rarity] || .2) + (ship.baseRarity && rarity !== ship.baseRarity ? .5 : 0);
  }
  const battleAudioPools=[];
  const battleAudioPriorityUntil=[0,0,0,0];
  let battleAudioMixTimer=null;
  function refreshBattleAudioMix(){
    const now=performance.now();let priority=0,nextExpiry=Infinity;
    for(let level=1;level<=3;level+=1){if(battleAudioPriorityUntil[level]>now){priority=Math.max(priority,level);nextExpiry=Math.min(nextExpiry,battleAudioPriorityUntil[level]);}}
    const duckFactor=priority===3?.14:priority===2?.24:priority===1?.4:1;
    battleAudioPools.forEach(pool=>pool.voices.forEach(audio=>{audio.volume=pool.baseVolume*(priority>pool.priority?duckFactor:1);}));
    clearTimeout(battleAudioMixTimer);
    if(Number.isFinite(nextExpiry))battleAudioMixTimer=setTimeout(refreshBattleAudioMix,Math.max(0,nextExpiry-now)+8);
  }
  function registerBattleAudioPool(voices,baseVolume,priority=0){battleAudioPools.push({voices,baseVolume,priority});refreshBattleAudioMix();}
  const battleLoseAudio=window.createGameAudio('sound/lose.mp3');battleLoseAudio.preload='auto';battleLoseAudio.volume=.65;
  const battleLoseVoices=Array.from({length:2},()=>battleLoseAudio.cloneNode());registerBattleAudioPool(battleLoseVoices,.65,2);
  let battleLoseVoiceIndex=0;
  function playBattleLoseSound(){const audio=battleLoseVoices[battleLoseVoiceIndex++%battleLoseVoices.length];try{audio.currentTime=0;audio.play().catch(()=>{});}catch{}}
  function focusBattleAudio(priority){
    const settings={retreat:[1,950],core:[2,1350],boss:[3,2100]}[priority];if(!settings)return;
    const [level,duration]=settings;battleAudioPriorityUntil[level]=Math.max(battleAudioPriorityUntil[level],performance.now()+duration);refreshBattleAudioMix();
  }
  const bossRoarAudio=window.createGameAudio('sound/boss_roar.wav');bossRoarAudio.preload='auto';bossRoarAudio.volume=.72;
  const bossRoarVoices=Array.from({length:2},()=>bossRoarAudio.cloneNode());registerBattleAudioPool(bossRoarVoices,.72,3);
  const bossKillAudio=window.createGameAudio('sound/boss_kill.wav');bossKillAudio.preload='auto';bossKillAudio.volume=.72;
  const bossKillVoices=Array.from({length:2},()=>bossKillAudio.cloneNode());registerBattleAudioPool(bossKillVoices,.72,3);
  const bossFireAudio=window.createGameAudio('sound/boss_fire.wav');bossFireAudio.preload='auto';bossFireAudio.volume=.62;
  const bossFireVoices=Array.from({length:4},()=>bossFireAudio.cloneNode());registerBattleAudioPool(bossFireVoices,.62,3);
  const bossBladeVolleyAudio=window.createGameAudio('sound/boss_blade_volley_8x.ogg');bossBladeVolleyAudio.preload='auto';bossBladeVolleyAudio.volume=.68;
  const bossBladeVolleyVoices=Array.from({length:2},()=>bossBladeVolleyAudio.cloneNode());registerBattleAudioPool(bossBladeVolleyVoices,.68,3);
  const poisonBombAudio=window.createGameAudio('sound/poison_bomb.wav');poisonBombAudio.preload='auto';poisonBombAudio.volume=.6;
  const poisonBombVoices=Array.from({length:3},()=>poisonBombAudio.cloneNode());registerBattleAudioPool(poisonBombVoices,.6,2);
  const midBossKillAudio=window.createGameAudio('sound/boss_mid_kill.wav');midBossKillAudio.preload='auto';midBossKillAudio.volume=.72;
  const midBossKillVoices=Array.from({length:2},()=>midBossKillAudio.cloneNode());registerBattleAudioPool(midBossKillVoices,.72,3);
  let bossRoarVoiceIndex=0,bossKillVoiceIndex=0,midBossKillVoiceIndex=0,bossFireVoiceIndex=0,bossBladeVolleyVoiceIndex=0,poisonBombVoiceIndex=0;
  function playBossRoarSound(){const audio=bossRoarVoices[bossRoarVoiceIndex++%bossRoarVoices.length];try{audio.currentTime=0;audio.play().catch(()=>{});}catch{}}
  function playBossKillSound(){const audio=bossKillVoices[bossKillVoiceIndex++%bossKillVoices.length];try{audio.currentTime=0;audio.play().catch(()=>{});}catch{}}

  }

  function playMonsterDeathSound(enemy){
    if(enemy?.monsterType==='plasma-bug'){
      const audio=plasmaBugDeathVoices[plasmaBugDeathVoiceIndex++%plasmaBugDeathVoices.length];
      try{audio.currentTime=0;audio.play().catch(()=>{});}catch{}
      return;
    }
    const archetype=enemy?.archetype||enemy?.monsterType;
    const voices=archetype==='gasimon'||archetype==='jipgemon'?gasimonDeathVoices:archetype==='dokchimmon'||archetype==='suicidemon'?dokchimmonDeathVoices:null;
    if(!voices)return;
    const index=archetype==='dokchimmon'||archetype==='suicidemon'?dokchimmonDeathVoiceIndex++:gasimonDeathVoiceIndex++;
    const audio=voices[index%voices.length];
    try{audio.currentTime=0;audio.play().catch(()=>{});}catch{}
  }
