    let mailAlert=portraitNode.querySelector('.captain-mail-alert');
    if(unreadMailCount()>0&&!mailAlert){mailAlert=document.createElement('span');mailAlert.className='captain-mail-alert';mailAlert.setAttribute('aria-label','읽지 않은 메일');mailAlert.textContent='!';portraitNode.append(mailAlert);}
    else if(unreadMailCount()===0)mailAlert?.remove();
    const rank={R:1,SR:2,SSR:3,L:4};
    const owned=window.getOwnedFleetVariants?.()||[];
    const best=owned.reduce((a,b)=>!a||(rank[b.rarity]||0)>(rank[a.rarity]||0)||((rank[b.rarity]||0)===(rank[a.rarity]||0)&&count(b.stars)>count(a.stars))?b:a,null);
    const fleet=hud.querySelector('[data-captain-fleet]');
    fleet.textContent=best?.name||'미보유';
    fleet.dataset.rarity=best?.rarity||'';
    fleet.parentElement.title=best?`최고등급 보유 함선 · ${best.name}`:'보유 함선 없음';
    const research=window.getCommanderResearchSummary?.()||[];
    const bestResearch=research[0];
    const weapon=hud.querySelector('[data-captain-weapon]');
    weapon.innerHTML=bestResearch?`<span class="captain-field-badge" title="${bestResearch.name} 연구">${bestResearch.badge||bestResearch.icon}</span><b>${bestResearch.stage}단계</b>`:'<span class="captain-field-none">—</span>';
    weapon.title=bestResearch?`${bestResearch.name} · ${bestResearch.node} ${bestResearch.stage}단계`:'무기 미개발';
    hud.querySelector('[data-captain-total]').textContent=`증표 ${format(total)}`;
    const balances=window.gameResources||{};
    hud.querySelectorAll('[data-captain-balance]').forEach(element=>{element.textContent=format(balances[element.dataset.captainBalance]);});
    const loot=window.getExplorationLootInventory?.()||{};
    hud.querySelectorAll('[data-captain-loot]').forEach(element=>{element.textContent=format(loot[element.dataset.captainLoot]);});
  }
  function close(){const previous=overlay?.opener;overlay?.remove();overlay=null;previous?.focus();window.dispatchEvent(new Event('captain-profile-closed'));}
  function paintDialog(view='profile'){
    if(!overlay)return;
    overlay.dataset.profileView=view;
    delete overlay.dataset.settingsActive;
    if(view==='settings'){window.openCaptainSettings(overlay,()=>paintDialog('profile'));return;}
    const character=profile.character,fullName=`Capt. ${character}${profile.lastName?' '+profile.lastName:''}`;
    if(view==='edit'){
      overlay.innerHTML=`<form class="captain-select-dialog" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="captain-select-title"><header><div><small>UNITED STAR FLEET</small><h2 id="captain-select-title">플레이어 네임 변경</h2></div><button type="button" data-captain-close aria-label="닫기">×</button></header><div class="captain-edit-identity">${portrait(character)}<strong>Capt. ${character}</strong></div><label for="captain-last-name">성 (Last name)</label><input id="captain-last-name" name="lastName" maxlength="16" required placeholder="성을 입력하세요" autocomplete="off" /><div class="captain-name-preview" aria-live="polite"></div><p class="captain-input-hint">한글·영문·숫자, 공백·하이픈·작은따옴표 · 최대 16자</p><div class="captain-form-error" role="alert"></div><button class="captain-save" type="submit">변경 저장</button></form>`;
      const input=overlay.querySelector('input');input.value=profile.lastName;
      const preview=()=>{overlay.querySelector('.captain-name-preview').textContent=`Capt. ${character} ${input.value.trim()||'플레이어 네임'}`;};
      preview();input.addEventListener('input',preview);
      overlay.querySelector('form').addEventListener('submit',event=>{
        event.preventDefault();const lastName=input.value.trim().replace(/\s+/g,' ');
        if(!/^[\p{L}\p{N} '-]{1,16}$/u.test(lastName)){overlay.querySelector('.captain-form-error').textContent='성을 1~16자로 입력해주세요.';input.focus();return;}
        const next={character,lastName};
        try{localStorage.setItem(PROFILE_KEY,JSON.stringify(next));}catch{overlay.querySelector('.captain-form-error').textContent='설정을 저장할 수 없습니다. 다시 시도해주세요.';return;}
        profile=next;profileLocked=true;render();paintDialog('profile');
      });
      input.focus();return;
    }
    const resourceCount=count((window.gameResources||{}).lionToken);
    const content=view==='mail'?mailListMarkup()
      :view==='membership'?`<div class="captain-info-view"><span class="captain-info-icon">✦</span><strong>Membership</strong><p class="captain-membership-state">${resourceCount>0?'거래 자격 보유':'사자증표가 있어야 거래할 수 있습니다.'}</p><div class="captain-membership-balance"><img src="icons/lion-token.png" alt="" />사자증표 <b>${format(resourceCount)}개</b></div><button type="button" class="captain-save" data-open-membership>Membership Service 열기</button></div>`
      :`<div class="captain-profile-summary">${portrait(character)}<div><small>COMMANDER DOSSIER</small><div class="captain-edit-name"><strong>${fullName}</strong><button type="button" class="captain-pencil" data-edit-name aria-label="이름 변경">✎</button></div><span>연합 함대 · 함장</span><em class="captain-clearance">UNITED STAR FLEET</em></div></div><div class="captain-profile-stats"><div><small>보유 함선</small><b>${format((window.getOwnedFleetVariants?.()||[]).length)}<em>척</em></b></div><div><small>누적 획득 사자증표</small><b>${format(total)}<em>개</em></b></div></div><div class="captain-profile-actions"><button type="button" data-profile-view="settings"><span>⚙</span><b>설정</b><small>SYSTEM</small></button><button type="button" class="captain-profile-mail-button" data-profile-view="mail" ${unreadMailCount()?`aria-label="메일 · 읽지 않은 메일 ${format(unreadMailCount())}건"`:''}><span>✉</span><b>메일</b><small>INBOX</small>${unreadMailCount()?'<i class="captain-profile-mail-alert" aria-hidden="true">!</i>':''}</button><button type="button" data-profile-view="membership"><span>✦</span><b>Membership</b><small>MEMBER SERVICE</small></button></div>`;
    overlay.innerHTML=`<section class="captain-select-dialog captain-profile-dialog" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="captain-select-title"><header><div><small>UNITED STAR FLEET</small><h2 id="captain-select-title">${view==='profile'?'함장 프로필':view==='settings'?'설정':view==='mail'?'메일함':'Membership'}</h2></div><button type="button" data-captain-close aria-label="닫기">×</button></header>${content}</section>`;
    overlay.querySelector('[data-edit-name]')?.addEventListener('click',()=>paintDialog('edit'));
    overlay.querySelectorAll('[data-profile-view]').forEach(button=>button.addEventListener('click',()=>paintDialog(button.dataset.profileView)));
    overlay.querySelectorAll('[data-mail-open]').forEach(button=>button.addEventListener('click',()=>paintMailDetail(button.dataset.mailOpen)));
    overlay.querySelector('[data-open-membership]')?.addEventListener('click',()=>{close();window.openAlienShop?.('featured');setTimeout(()=>document.querySelector('.alien-shop-token-stall')?.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth',block:'center'}),80);});
    overlay.querySelector('[data-captain-close]')?.focus();
  }
  function open(){
    if(overlay)return;
    overlay=document.createElement('div');overlay.className='captain-select-overlay';overlay.opener=document.activeElement;
    document.body.append(overlay);
    overlay.addEventListener('click',event=>{
      if(event.target===overlay){
        if(profileLocked&&(overlay.dataset.settingsActive==='true'||overlay.dataset.profileView!=='profile'))paintDialog('profile');
        else close();
        return;
      }
      if(event.target.closest('[data-captain-close]')){
        if(overlay.dataset.settingsActive==='true')return;
        if(overlay.dataset.profileView==='mail-detail'){paintDialog('mail');return;}
        if(profileLocked&&overlay.dataset.profileView!=='profile')paintDialog('profile');
        else close();
      }
    });
    overlay.addEventListener('keydown',event=>{
      if(event.key==='Escape'){
        event.preventDefault();
        if(overlay.dataset.settingsActive==='true')overlay.querySelector('[data-captain-close]')?.click();
        else if(profileLocked&&overlay.dataset.profileView!=='profile')paintDialog('profile');
        else close();
        return;
      }
      if(event.key==='Tab'){
        const controls=[...overlay.querySelectorAll('button:not(:disabled),input:not(:disabled),a[href]')].filter(node=>node.getClientRects().length);
        const first=controls[0],last=controls[controls.length-1];
        if(event.shiftKey&&document.activeElement===first){event.preventDefault();last?.focus();}
        else if(!event.shiftKey&&document.activeElement===last){event.preventDefault();first?.focus();}
      }
    });
    paintDialog(profileLocked?'profile':'setup');
    if(!profileLocked){
      const character=profile.character;
      overlay.innerHTML=`<form class="captain-select-dialog" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="captain-select-title"><header><div><small>UNITED STAR FLEET</small><h2 id="captain-select-title">함장 프로필</h2></div><button type="button" data-captain-close aria-label="닫기">×</button></header><p>당신의 함장을 선택하세요.</p><div class="captain-candidates" role="group" aria-label="주인공 선택">${Object.keys(characters).map(name=>`<button type="button" data-character="${name}" aria-pressed="${name===character}">${portrait(name)}<strong>${name}</strong><small>FLEET CAPTAIN</small></button>`).join('')}</div><label for="captain-last-name">플레이어 네임 · 성 (Last name)</label><input id="captain-last-name" name="lastName" maxlength="16" required placeholder="성을 입력하세요" autocomplete="off" /><div class="captain-name-preview" aria-live="polite"></div><p class="captain-input-hint">한글·영문·숫자, 공백·하이픈·작은따옴표 · 최대 16자</p><div class="captain-form-error" role="alert"></div><button class="captain-save" type="submit">함장 설정 완료</button></form>`;
      let selected=character;const input=overlay.querySelector('input');input.value=profile.lastName;
      const preview=()=>{overlay.querySelector('.captain-name-preview').textContent=`Capt. ${selected} ${input.value.trim()||'플레이어 네임'}`;};preview();input.addEventListener('input',preview);
      overlay.addEventListener('click',event=>{const candidate=event.target.closest('[data-character]');if(candidate){selected=candidate.dataset.character;overlay.querySelectorAll('[data-character]').forEach(button=>button.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(button===candidate)));preview();}});
      overlay.querySelector('form').addEventListener('submit',event=>{event.preventDefault();const lastName=input.value.trim().replace(/\s+/g,' ');if(!/^[\p{L}\p{N} '-]{1,16}$/u.test(lastName)){overlay.querySelector('.captain-form-error').textContent='성을 1~16자로 입력해주세요.';input.focus();return;}const next={character:selected,lastName};try{localStorage.setItem(PROFILE_KEY,JSON.stringify(next));}catch{overlay.querySelector('.captain-form-error').textContent='설정을 저장할 수 없습니다. 다시 시도해주세요.';return;}profile=next;profileLocked=true;render();paintDialog('profile');window.dispatchEvent(new Event('captain-profile-created'));});
      input.focus();
    }
  }
  window.openCaptainMailbox=()=>{if(!overlay)open();if(overlay)paintDialog('mail');};
  window.closeCaptainProfile=close;
  window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>{
    hud=document.querySelector('#persistent-hud .fixed-top');if(!hud)return;
    const balances=window.gameResources||(window.gameResources={gold:1240,thorium:86,lionToken:0});
    if(!window.__inventoryBalancesHydrated){Object.assign(balances,read(RESOURCE_KEY)||{});window.__inventoryBalancesHydrated=true;}
    total=Math.max(total,count(balances.lionToken));persistTotal();
    hud.classList.add('captain-hud');
    hud.innerHTML=`<button type="button" class="captain-profile-open" aria-label="함장 프로필">${portrait(profile.character)}<span class="captain-profile-copy"><strong class="captain-name"></strong><span class="captain-credentials"><span class="captain-ship-line" title="보유 최고등급 함선"><span data-captain-fleet></span><span class="captain-weapon-stage" data-captain-weapon></span></span><span class="captain-lifetime" title="누적 획득 사자증표 · 소비해도 유지됩니다"><img src="icons/lion-token.png" alt="" /><b data-captain-total>증표 0</b></span></span></span></button><div class="captain-balances alien-shop-balances" aria-label="보유 자원"><span class="captain-drone-balance" title="탐색드론"><img src="icons/scout-drone.png" alt="탐색드론" /><b data-captain-loot="scoutTron">0</b></span><span class="captain-gold-balance" title="골드"><img src="gold-resource-icon.svg" alt="골드" /><b data-captain-balance="gold">0</b></span><span class="captain-precision-drone-balance" data-item-id="precisionScoutDrone" title="정밀탐색드론 · 추후 함종별 함선 탐색에 사용"><img src="${PRECISION_SCOUT_DRONE_ICON}" alt="정밀탐색드론" /><b>0</b></span><span class="captain-thorium-balance" title="토륨"><img src="thorium-crystal-icon.png" alt="토륨" /><b data-captain-balance="thorium">0</b></span><span class="captain-staff-balance" title="연구인력"><img src="icons/research-personnel-20261002.png" alt="연구인력" /><b data-captain-loot="researchStaff">0</b></span><span class="captain-token-balance" title="사자증표"><img src="icons/lion-token.png" alt="사자증표" /><b data-captain-balance="lionToken">0</b></span></div>`;
    hud.querySelector('.captain-profile-open').addEventListener('click',open);
    ensureWelcomeMail();
    const oldUpdate=window.updateGameResourceHud;
    window.updateGameResourceHud=()=>{oldUpdate?.();render();};
    ['game-resources-updated','fleet-ownership-updated','commander-research-updated','tactical-research-updated'].forEach(event=>window.addEventListener(event,render));
    window.addEventListener('captain-mail-updated',()=>{render();if(overlay?.dataset.profileView==='mail'||overlay?.dataset.profileView==='profile')paintDialog(overlay.dataset.profileView);});
    window.addEventListener('storage',event=>{if(event.key===TOTAL_KEY)total=count(read(TOTAL_KEY)?.earned);render();if(event.key===MAIL_KEY&&(overlay?.dataset.profileView==='mail'||overlay?.dataset.profileView==='profile'))paintDialog(overlay.dataset.profileView);});
    render();
    if(!profileLocked)open();
  });
})();

/* ===== FILE: eva9-tutorial.js ===== */
(()=>{
  'use strict';
  const localPrototype=location.protocol==='file:'||['localhost','127.0.0.1'].includes(location.hostname);
  if(localPrototype&&new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('tutorial')!=='1')return;
  const KEY='starfleet-eva9-tutorial-v1';
  const FARAH_KEY='starfleet-farah-tutorial-v1';
  const MAIL_KEY='starfleet-captain-mail-v1';
  const LOOT_KEY='starfleet-exploration-loot-v1';
  const RESOURCE_KEY='starfleet-game-resources-v1';
  const PROFILE_KEY='starfleet-commander-profile-v1';
  const SUPPORT_MAIL='eva9-research-support-v1';
  const COMPLETION_MAIL='eva9-research-completion-v1';
  const read=key=>{try{return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key)||'null');}catch{return null;}};
  const save=()=>{try{localStorage.setItem(KEY,JSON.stringify(state));}catch{}};
  const count=value=>Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(value)||0));
  let state={step:'',mailId:'',dialogueCollapsed:false,...(read(KEY)||{})};
  let panel,chip;
  const active=()=>!!state.step&&state.step!=='complete';
  const farahFinished=()=>read(FARAH_KEY)?.step==='complete'&&count(read('planet-campaign-v1')?.mars)>=5;
  const playerName=()=>{const profile=read(PROFILE_KEY)||{};return [profile.character,profile.lastName].filter(Boolean).join(' ')||'신임';};
  const mailList=()=>Array.isArray(read(MAIL_KEY))?read(MAIL_KEY):[];
  const unclaimed=mail=>mail&&!mail.claimedAt&&Object.values(mail.rewards||{}).some(value=>count(value)>0);
  const pendingMail=()=>mailList().find(mail=>mail.id===state.mailId&&unclaimed(mail))||mailList().find(mail=>mail.id==='farah-mars-5-congratulations-v1'&&unclaimed(mail))||mailList().find(mail=>mail.id==='welcome-gift-2026-01'&&unclaimed(mail))||mailList().find(unclaimed);
  const inventory=()=>({...(read(LOOT_KEY)||{}),...(window.getExplorationLootInventory?.()||{})});
  const resources=()=>({...(read(RESOURCE_KEY)||{}),...(window.gameResources||{})});
  const ensureMailSupplies=()=>{
    const available=pendingMail(),loot=inventory(),balance=resources(),rewards=available?.rewards||{};
    const shortage={scoutTron:Math.max(0,10-count(loot.scoutTron)-count(rewards.scoutTron)),researchStaff:Math.max(0,10-count(loot.researchStaff)-count(rewards.researchStaff)),gold:Math.max(0,1000-count(balance.gold)-count(rewards.gold))};
    if(Object.values(shortage).some(Boolean)&&!mailList().some(mail=>mail.id===SUPPORT_MAIL)){
      const support={scoutTron:Math.max(0,10-count(loot.scoutTron)),researchStaff:Math.max(0,10-count(loot.researchStaff)),gold:Math.max(0,1000-count(balance.gold))};
      window.deliverCaptainMail?.({id:SUPPORT_MAIL,title:'EVA-9 · 연구실 실습 보급',sender:'EVA-9 · 연구실 운영관',body:'전함 탐색과 전술무기 연구 실습에 필요한 부족분을 보급합니다.',rewards:support});
    }
    const selected=mailList().find(mail=>mail.id===SUPPORT_MAIL&&unclaimed(mail))||pendingMail();
    state.mailId=selected?.id||'';save();
    return selected;
  };
  const lines={
    intro:()=>({title:'EVA-9 · 연구실 안내',body:pendingMail()?`안녕하세요, ${playerName()} 함장님. 연구실 관련 안내를 해드리겠습니다. 먼저 메일을 확인하고 보급품을 수령하세요.`:`안녕하세요, ${playerName()} 함장님. 보급품은 이미 수령하셨군요. 연구실 관련 안내를 해드리겠습니다.`,action:pendingMail()?'메일 확인하기':'연구실 안내 시작'}),
    profileOpen:()=>({title:'함장 프로필',body:'화면의 함장 프로필 사진을 누르세요.'}),
    mailOpen:()=>({title:'메일함',body:'함장 프로필에서 메일 버튼을 선택하세요.'}),
    mailSelect:()=>({title:'보급 메일 확인',body:'보급품이 첨부된 메일을 열어 주세요.'}),
    mailClaim:()=>({title:'보급품 수령',body:'메일에 첨부된 보급품을 모두 수령하세요.'}),
    labPrompt:()=>({title:'연구실로 이동',body:'연구실로 이동하겠습니다.',action:'연구실로 이동'}),
    explore10:()=>({title:'전함 탐색',body:'전력 증강을 위해 새로운 함선을 탐색하겠습니다. 성간을 탐색하여 사용할 수 있는 함선을 찾아내면 신호를 보내주는 고성능 드론입니다. 1대 혹은 10대씩 보낼 수 있습니다. 보급받은 드론으로 10회 탐색을 해보세요.'}),
    exploreConfirm:()=>({title:'탐색 확인',body:'탐색드론 10대를 보내는 것을 확정해 주세요.'}),
    exploreResult:()=>({title:'탐색 결과 확인',body:'발견한 함선과 보급품을 확인하세요. 마지막 결과까지 본 뒤 돌아가기를 누르세요.'}),
    weaponTab:()=>({title:'전술무기 연구',body:'이번에는 전술무기연구를 알려드리겠습니다. 연구실의 무기 연구 탭을 누르세요.'}),
    weapon10:()=>({title:'10회 연구',body:'연구인력 10명을 투입하여 전술무기를 10회 연구해 보세요.'}),
    weaponResult:()=>({title:'연구 결과',body:'획득한 연구자료를 확인한 뒤 확인 버튼을 누르세요.'}),
    frigateOpen:()=>({title:'프리깃 사용 개방',body:'연구에는 인력과 자원이 필요하죠. 연구를 통해 확보한 연구자료와 연구코어로 우리 함대의 무기와 방어체계를 강화할 수 있습니다. 먼저, 중요한 전력인 프리깃을 강화하겠습니다. 프리깃의 개발 버튼을 누르세요.'}),
    frigateNode:()=>({title:'개발 항목 선택',body:'계통도에서 「프리깃 사용 개방」을 선택하세요.'}),
    frigateDevelop:()=>({title:'프리깃 개발',body:'「프리깃 사용 개방 개발」 버튼을 눌러 편성 슬롯을 여세요.'}),
    frigateClose:()=>({title:'개발 완료',body:'개발 창의 × 버튼을 눌러 연구실로 돌아가세요.'}),
    fleetNav:()=>({title:'함대관리로 이동',body:'이제 함대에 프리깃 함선을 증원할 수 있습니다. 아래 함대관리 버튼을 누르세요.'}),
    frigateSlot:()=>({title:'프리깃 슬롯',body:'편성 화면에서 프리깃 슬롯을 누르세요.'}),
    littleBoy:()=>({title:'리틀보이 출전',body:'엔지니어 Farah에게 받으신 리틀보이를 출전시키겠습니다. 목록에서 리틀보이를 선택하세요.'}),
    final:()=>({title:'연구실 안내 완료',body:'연구실은 함대 전력을 강화하는 데 핵심적인 역할을 하고 있습니다. 또 뵙겠습니다, 함장님.',action:'안내 완료'})
  };
  const setStep=step=>{state.step=step;save();paint();};
  const deliverCompletionGift=()=>window.deliverCaptainMail?.({id:COMPLETION_MAIL,title:'EVA-9 · 연구실 교육 완료 선물',sender:'EVA-9 · 연구실 운영관',body:'전함 탐색과 전술무기 연구 교육을 완료하셨습니다. 다음 함대 개발에 필요한 보급품을 보내드립니다.',rewards:{gold:2000,thorium:100,scoutTron:10,researchStaff:10}});
  const finish=()=>{deliverCompletionGift();setStep('complete');window.notice?.('EVA-9 연구실 튜토리얼을 마쳤습니다. 선물이 메일함에 도착했습니다.');window.dispatchEvent(new Event('eva9-tutorial-completed'));};
  const paint=()=>{
    if(!panel)return;
    const line=lines[state.step]?.();panel.hidden=!line;
    panel.classList.toggle('dialogue-collapsed',!!line&&!!state.dialogueCollapsed);
    chip.hidden=!line||!state.dialogueCollapsed;
    document.body.dataset.evaStep=line?state.step:'';
    if(!line)return;
    panel.dataset.step=state.step;
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-mail-open]').forEach(row=>row.classList.toggle('eva-mail-target',state.step==='mailSelect'&&row.dataset.mailOpen===state.mailId));
    panel.querySelector('[data-eva-title]').textContent=line.title;
    panel.querySelector('[data-eva-body]').textContent=line.body;
    const action=panel.querySelector('[data-eva-action]');action.hidden=!line.action;action.textContent=line.action||'';
    const toggle=panel.querySelector('[data-eva-portrait-toggle]');toggle.textContent='대화창 내리기';toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded',String(!state.dialogueCollapsed));
  };
  const start=()=>{
    if(state.step||!farahFinished())return;
    ensureMailSupplies();
    window.show?.('home');
    setStep('intro');
  };
  const nextAfterMail=()=>{window.closeCaptainProfile?.();setStep('labPrompt');};
  const doAction=()=>{
    if(state.step==='intro'){
      if(ensureMailSupplies())setStep('profileOpen');else setStep('labPrompt');
    }else if(state.step==='labPrompt'){
      window.closeCaptainProfile?.();
      if(window.openResearchLab?.()!==false){window.summonTab?.('ship');setStep('explore10');}
    }else if(state.step==='final')finish();
  };
  const allowClick=target=>{
    if(target.closest('.eva9-tutorial,.eva9-tutorial-chip'))return true;
    if(state.step==='profileOpen')return !!target.closest('.captain-profile-open');
    if(state.step==='mailOpen')return !!target.closest('.captain-profile-mail-button');
    if(state.step==='mailSelect')return target.closest('[data-mail-open]')?.dataset.mailOpen===state.mailId;
    if(state.step==='mailClaim')return !!target.closest('.captain-mail-claim');
    if(state.step==='explore10')return !!target.closest('#ship-pool .summon-action.ten');
    if(state.step==='exploreConfirm')return !!target.closest('.ship-explore-confirm');
    if(state.step==='exploreResult')return !!target.closest('.ship-exploration-card-wrap,.ship-explore-return');
    if(state.step==='weaponTab')return !!target.closest('.research-tab[data-research="weapon"]');
    if(state.step==='weapon10')return !!target.closest('[data-tactical-pull="10"]');
    if(state.step==='weaponResult')return !!target.closest('.tactical-research-finish');
    if(state.step==='frigateOpen')return !!target.closest('[data-tactical-develop="frigate"]');
    if(state.step==='frigateNode')return !!target.closest('[data-branch-node="firepower1"]');
    if(state.step==='frigateDevelop')return !!target.closest('.tactical-tech-overlay .cannon-tech-action');
    if(state.step==='frigateClose')return !!target.closest('.tactical-tech-overlay .tactical-tech-close');
    if(state.step==='fleetNav')return !!target.closest('.fixed-nav [data-screen="collection"]');
    if(state.step==='frigateSlot')return !!target.closest('#collection [data-fleet-slot="3"]');
    if(state.step==='littleBoy')return !!target.closest('#collection .fleet-ship-choice[data-ship-name="리틀보이"]');
    return false;
  };
  const previousAllowsScreen=window.tutorialAllowsScreen;
  window.tutorialAllowsScreen=id=>{
    if(previousAllowsScreen&&!previousAllowsScreen(id))return false;
    if(!active())return true;
    if(['intro','profileOpen','mailOpen','mailSelect','mailClaim','labPrompt'].includes(state.step))return id==='home'||id==='summon'&&state.step==='labPrompt';
    if(['fleetNav','frigateSlot','littleBoy','final'].includes(state.step))return id==='collection'||id==='summon'&&state.step==='fleetNav';
    return id==='summon';
  };
  document.addEventListener('click',event=>{
    if(!active()||allowClick(event.target))return;
    event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();window.notice?.('EVA-9의 현재 안내 단계를 먼저 완료해 주세요.');
  },true);
  document.addEventListener('click',event=>{
    if(!active())return;
    const target=event.target;
    if(state.step==='profileOpen'&&target.closest('.captain-profile-open'))setStep('mailOpen');
    else if(state.step==='mailOpen'&&target.closest('.captain-profile-mail-button'))setStep('mailSelect');
    else if(state.step==='explore10'&&target.closest('#ship-pool .summon-action.ten'))setStep('exploreConfirm');
    else if(state.step==='exploreResult'&&target.closest('.ship-explore-return'))setStep('weaponTab');
    else if(state.step==='weaponTab'&&target.closest('.research-tab[data-research="weapon"]'))setStep('weapon10');
    else if(state.step==='weaponResult'&&target.closest('.tactical-research-finish'))setStep('frigateOpen');
    else if(state.step==='frigateOpen'&&target.closest('[data-tactical-develop="frigate"]'))setStep('frigateNode');
    else if(state.step==='frigateNode'&&target.closest('[data-branch-node="firepower1"]'))setStep('frigateDevelop');
    else if(state.step==='frigateClose'&&target.closest('.tactical-tech-overlay .tactical-tech-close'))setStep('fleetNav');
    else if(state.step==='frigateSlot'&&target.closest('#collection [data-fleet-slot="3"]'))setStep('littleBoy');
  });
  window.addEventListener('captain-mail-opened',event=>{if(state.step==='mailSelect'&&event.detail?.id===state.mailId)setStep('mailClaim');});
  window.addEventListener('captain-mail-claimed',event=>{if(state.step==='mailClaim'&&event.detail?.id===state.mailId)nextAfterMail();});
  window.addEventListener('ship-exploration-completed',event=>{if(state.step==='exploreConfirm'&&Number(event.detail?.count)===10)setStep('exploreResult');});
  window.addEventListener('weapon-research-completed',event=>{if(state.step==='weapon10'&&Number(event.detail?.count)===10)setStep('weaponResult');});
  window.addEventListener('tactical-research-updated',event=>{if(state.step==='frigateDevelop'&&event.detail?.field==='frigate'&&Number(event.detail?.level)>=1)setStep('frigateClose');});
  window.addEventListener('game-screen-changed',event=>{if(state.step==='fleetNav'&&event.detail?.screen==='collection')setStep('frigateSlot');});
  window.addEventListener('fleet-slot-assigned',event=>{if(state.step==='littleBoy'&&event.detail?.slot===3&&event.detail?.ship==='리틀보이')setStep('final');});
  window.addEventListener('farah-tutorial-completed',()=>setTimeout(start,0));
  window.addEventListener('campaign-gate-cleared',()=>setTimeout(start,0));
  window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>{
    panel=document.createElement('aside');panel.className='eva9-tutorial';panel.hidden=true;panel.setAttribute('aria-label','EVA-9 연구실 튜토리얼');
    panel.innerHTML='<img src="eva9-tutorial-bust.png" alt="EVA-9 흉상"/><div class="eva9-dialogue"><div class="eva9-dialogue-head"><small>SYNTHETIC OPERATOR · EVA-9</small><button type="button" data-eva-portrait-toggle aria-expanded="true">대화창 내리기</button></div><strong data-eva-title></strong><p data-eva-body></p><div class="eva9-actions"><button type="button" data-eva-action hidden></button></div></div>';
    chip=document.createElement('button');chip.type='button';chip.className='eva9-tutorial-chip';chip.textContent='튜토리얼 보기';chip.hidden=true;chip.setAttribute('aria-label','EVA-9 튜토리얼 보기');
    document.body.append(panel,chip);
    panel.querySelector('[data-eva-action]').onclick=doAction;
    panel.querySelector('[data-eva-portrait-toggle]').onclick=()=>{state.dialogueCollapsed=true;save();paint();};
    chip.onclick=()=>{state.dialogueCollapsed=false;save();paint();};
    if(state.step==='exploreConfirm')state.step='explore10';
    if(state.step==='exploreResult')state.step='weaponTab';
    if(state.step==='weaponResult')state.step='frigateOpen';
    if(['frigateNode','frigateDevelop','frigateClose'].includes(state.step))state.step='frigateOpen';
    if(['mailOpen','mailSelect','mailClaim'].includes(state.step)){
      const mail=pendingMail();state.mailId=mail?.id||'';state.step=mail?'profileOpen':'labPrompt';
    }
    if(state.step==='littleBoy')state.step=window.getFleetAssignments?.()[3]?.name==='리틀보이'?'final':'frigateSlot';
    save();paint();
    if(state.step==='complete')deliverCompletionGift();
    setTimeout(()=>{
      if(!state.step){start();return;}
      if(!active())return;
      if(['explore10','weaponTab','weapon10','frigateOpen'].includes(state.step))window.openResearchLab?.();
      if(['frigateSlot','littleBoy','final'].includes(state.step))window.openFleetManagement?.();
    },120);
  });
})();

(()=>{

    lines[`prep${gate}`]={mood:'focused',title:`화성 ${gate}관문 · 전투 준비`,body:'중앙의 전투개시 버튼을 눌러 줘. 전투는 함선이 자동으로 진행해.'};

  }
  window.tutorialAllowsScreen=id=>{
    if(!active())return true;

    window.acquireFleetShip?.(name);
    const after=Number(window.getFleetCatalog?.().find(ship=>ship.name===name)?.copies)||0;
    if(after<=before){window.notice?.(`${name} 보급을 저장하지 못했습니다.`);return false;}
    state.grants[gate]=true;save();return true;
  };
  const skipRemainingTutorial=()=>{
    // EVA-9 later requires Little Boy in the frigate slot. Skipping Farah must

    if(!grantGateShip(2)||!grantGateShip(3))return;

  };

  };

    if(state.step==='profile')return !!target.closest('.captain-select-overlay form')&&!target.closest('[data-captain-close]');

  });

  window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>{
    panel=document.createElement('aside');panel.className='farah-tutorial';panel.hidden=true;panel.setAttribute('aria-label','파라 나디르 튜토리얼');
    panel.innerHTML='<img src="farah-nadir-confident.png" alt="웃는 파라 나디르"/><div class="farah-dialogue"><div class="farah-dialogue-head"><small>CHIEF MECHANIC · FARAH NADIR</small><button type="button" data-farah-portrait-toggle aria-expanded="true">대화창 내리기</button></div><strong data-farah-title></strong><p data-farah-body></p><div class="farah-actions"><button type="button" data-farah-action hidden></button><button type="button" data-farah-secondary hidden></button></div></div>';
    chip=document.createElement('button');chip.type='button';chip.className='farah-tutorial-chip';chip.textContent='튜토리얼 보기';chip.hidden=true;chip.setAttribute('aria-label','파라 튜토리얼 보기');
    reveal=document.createElement('div');reveal.className='farah-ship-reveal';reveal.hidden=true;
    reveal.innerHTML='<div class="farah-ship-reveal-card" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="farah-ship-title"><small>FIRST FLEET ACQUIRED</small><div class="farah-reveal-art"><img src="battlecruiser-ship-art.png" alt="스타 크루저"/></div><h2 id="farah-ship-title">스타 크루저 획득</h2><p>첫 함선 · 0성</p><button type="button" data-farah-reveal-continue>함선 확인</button></div>';
    document.body.append(panel,chip,reveal);
    panel.querySelector('[data-farah-action]').onclick=doAction;
    panel.querySelector('[data-farah-secondary]').onclick=skipRemainingTutorial;
    panel.querySelector('[data-farah-portrait-toggle]').onclick=()=>{dialogueCollapsed=true;try{localStorage.setItem(COLLAPSED_KEY,'true');}catch{}paint();};
    chip.onclick=()=>{dialogueCollapsed=false;try{localStorage.setItem(COLLAPSED_KEY,'false');}catch{}paint();};
    reveal.querySelector('[data-farah-reveal-continue]').onclick=()=>setStep('intro');
    const image=window.getFleetCatalog?.().find(ship=>ship.name==='스타 크루저')?.image;if(image)reveal.querySelector('img').src=image;
    if(!state.step){state.step=profileReady()?'reveal':'profile';state.version=2;}
    if(/^prep[1-5]$|^combat[1-5]$/.test(state.step))state.step=`sortie${Number(state.step.match(/[1-5]/)?.[0])||1}`;
    if(state.step==='gate1Won')state.step='branch';
    if(state.step==='gate2Won')state.step='fleetTransit2';
    if(state.step==='gate3Won')state.step='fleetTransit3';
    if(state.step==='gate4Won')state.step='enhance';
    if(/^home[1-5]$/.test(state.step))state.step=state.step.replace('home','sortie');
    if(/^sortie[1-5]$/.test(state.step)&&!(window.getFleetAssignments?.()||[]).some(Boolean)){state.resumeGate=Number(state.step.match(/[1-5]/)?.[0])||1;state.step='chooseSlot';}
    if(state.step==='chooseShip'&&document.querySelector('#collection .formation-panel')?.hidden)state.step='chooseSlot';
    if(state.step==='profile'&&profileReady())state.step='reveal';
    save();paint();
    if(state.step==='complete'&&state.grants[1]&&(!state.grants[2]||!state.grants[3]))skipRemainingTutorial();
    if(['chooseSlot','branch','enhance','fleetTransit2','fleetTransit3'].includes(state.step))setTimeout(()=>window.openFleetManagement?.(),80);
  });
})();

/* ===== FILE: first-token-event.js ===== */
(() => {
  const claimKey = 'starfleet-first-token-event-v1';
  const resourceKey = 'starfleet-game-resources-v1';
  const eventImage = 'icons/first-token-event.png';
  const rewards = [
    ['battlecruiser-turtle-ship-codex.png', '배틀크루저 거북선', 1],
    ['icons/scout-drone.png?v=20260929-2', '탐색드론', 10],
    ['icons/research-personnel-20261002.png', '연구인력', 10],
    ['icons/ship-frame.png', '미사일 함선골조', 1]
  ];
  const claimed = () => localStorage.getItem(claimKey) === 'claimed';
  const eventCatalog = [
    { id: 'first-token', title: '생애 첫 증표', summary: '첫 증표 특별 보급', image: eventImage, isActive: () => true },
    { id: 'arena', title: 'Arena', summary: 'AI 대전 · 함대 리그', image: 'icons/arena-button.png', isActive: () => true }
  ];

  window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
    const deck = document.getElementById('command-deck');
    const toggle = deck?.querySelector('.deck-shortcut.events');
    if (!deck || !toggle) return;
    toggle.removeAttribute('onclick');
    toggle.type = 'button';
    toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
    toggle.setAttribute('aria-controls', 'deck-event-list');

    const list = document.createElement('div');
    list.id = 'deck-event-list';
    list.className = 'deck-event-list';
    list.setAttribute('aria-label', '이벤트 목록');
    list.hidden = true;
    list.innerHTML = '';
    document.body.append(list);

    const positionList = () => {
      if (list.hidden) return;
      const buttonRect = toggle.getBoundingClientRect();
      const margin = 12;
      const viewport = window.visualViewport;
      // A fixed menu belongs to the currently visible viewport. On mobile, the
      // visual viewport can move or shrink as the browser chrome changes size.
      const viewportLeft = viewport?.offsetLeft || 0;
      const viewportTop = viewport?.offsetTop || 0;
      const viewportWidth = viewport?.width || window.innerWidth;
      const viewportHeight = viewport?.height || window.innerHeight;
      const leftEdge = viewportLeft + margin;
      const topEdge = viewportTop + margin;
      const rightEdge = viewportLeft + viewportWidth - margin;
      const bottomEdge = viewportTop + viewportHeight - margin;
      const availableWidth = Math.max(1, rightEdge - leftEdge);
      const menuWidth = Math.min(204, availableWidth);
      const centeredLeft = buttonRect.left + buttonRect.width / 2 - menuWidth / 2;
      let left = Math.max(leftEdge, Math.min(rightEdge - menuWidth, centeredLeft));
      list.style.width = `${menuWidth}px`;
      list.style.boxSizing = 'border-box';
      list.style.overflowY = 'auto';
      list.style.maxHeight = `${Math.max(1, Math.min(280, viewportHeight * 0.42, viewportHeight - margin * 2))}px`;
      list.style.left = `${left}px`;
      const renderedRect = list.getBoundingClientRect();
      if (renderedRect.left < leftEdge) left += leftEdge - renderedRect.left;
      if (renderedRect.right > rightEdge) left -= renderedRect.right - rightEdge;
      left = Math.max(leftEdge, Math.min(rightEdge - menuWidth, left));
      list.style.left = `${left}px`;
      list.style.setProperty('--event-arrow-left', `${Math.max(10, Math.min(menuWidth - 20, buttonRect.left + buttonRect.width / 2 - left - 5))}px`);
      const menuHeight = list.offsetHeight;
      const below = buttonRect.bottom + 8;
      const above = buttonRect.top - menuHeight - 8;
      const preferredTop = below + menuHeight <= bottomEdge ? below : above >= topEdge ? above : below;
      const top = Math.max(topEdge, Math.min(bottomEdge - menuHeight, preferredTop));
      list.style.top = `${top}px`;
    };

    const closeList = () => { list.hidden = true; toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false'); };
    toggle.addEventListener('click', event => {
      event.stopPropagation();
      list.hidden = !list.hidden;
      toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(!list.hidden));
      positionList();
    });
    window.addEventListener('resize', positionList);
    window.addEventListener('scroll', positionList, true);
    window.visualViewport?.addEventListener('resize', positionList);
    window.visualViewport?.addEventListener('scroll', positionList);
    document.addEventListener('click', event => {
      if (!list.hidden && !list.contains(event.target) && !toggle.contains(event.target)) closeList();
    });

    const overlay = document.createElement('div');
    overlay.className = 'first-token-overlay';
    overlay.hidden = true;
    overlay.innerHTML = `<section class="first-token-dialog" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="first-token-title">
      <header><div><small>LIMITED EVENT · FIRST TOKEN</small><div class="first-token-title-row"><h2 id="first-token-title">생애 첫 증표</h2><b class="first-token-limit">계정당 1회</b></div></div><button type="button" class="first-token-close" aria-label="닫기">×</button></header>
      <div class="first-token-feature"><div class="first-token-ship"><img src="${rewards[0][0]}" alt="배틀크루저 거북선"/><b class="first-token-ssr-badge">SSR</b><button type="button" class="first-token-zoom" aria-label="거북선 상세정보 보기" title="상세정보 보기">⌕</button></div><div class="first-token-ship-name"><b>배틀크루저 거북선</b></div></div>
      <div class="first-token-rewards" aria-label="이벤트 보상">${rewards.slice(1).map(([image, name, count]) => `<div class="first-token-reward"><img src="${image}" alt="${name}"/><strong>×${count}</strong></div>`).join('')}</div>
      <p class="first-token-status" aria-live="polite"></p>
      <button type="button" class="first-token-buy"><span>특가 구매</span><span class="first-token-price"><img src="icons/lion-token.png" alt="사자증표"/> ×1</span></button>
    </section>`;
    document.body.append(overlay);
    const shortage = document.createElement('div');
    shortage.className = 'first-token-shortage';
    shortage.hidden = true;
    shortage.innerHTML = '<section role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="사자증표 부족"><span class="first-token-shortage-mark">!</span><b>사자증표가 부족합니다</b><small>암시장에서 사자증표를 준비할 수 있습니다.</small><button type="button">→ 상점이동</button></section>';
    document.body.append(shortage);
    const status = overlay.querySelector('.first-token-status');
    const buy = overlay.querySelector('.first-token-buy');
    const refresh = () => {
      const done = claimed();
      buy.disabled = done;
      buy.querySelector('span:first-child').textContent = done ? '구매 완료' : '특가 구매';
      status.textContent = done ? '구매 완료 · 보상은 보유함선과 물자창고에서 확인할 수 있습니다.' : '';
      const activeEvents = eventCatalog.filter(item => item.isActive());
      const badge = toggle.querySelector('.deck-event-active-icon');
      if (badge && activeEvents[0]) {
        badge.src = activeEvents[0].image;
        badge.alt = `${activeEvents[0].title} 아이콘`;
      }
      toggle.classList.toggle('no-active-events', activeEvents.length === 0);
      toggle.setAttribute('aria-label', activeEvents.length > 1 ? `진행 중 이벤트 ${activeEvents.length}개, 목록 열기` : activeEvents.length ? `${activeEvents[0].title} 이벤트 목록 열기` : '진행 중인 이벤트 없음');
      list.innerHTML = activeEvents.length
        ? `${activeEvents.length > 1 ? `<b class="deck-event-list-heading">진행 중 이벤트 · ${activeEvents.length}</b>` : ''}${activeEvents.map(item => `<button type="button" class="deck-event-entry${item.id === 'first-token' && done ? ' claimed' : ''}" data-event-id="${item.id}"><img src="${item.image}" alt=""/><span><b>${item.title}</b><small>${item.summary}</small></span><i aria-hidden="true">›</i></button>`).join('')}`
        : '<p class="deck-event-empty">현재 진행 중인 이벤트가 없습니다.</p>';
    };
    const close = () => { overlay.hidden = true; };
    shortage.addEventListener('click', event => {
      if (event.target === shortage) { shortage.hidden = true; return; }
      if (!event.target.closest('button')) return;
      shortage.hidden = true;
      close();
      window.openAlienShop?.('black-market');
    });
    list.addEventListener('click', event => {
      const entry = event.target.closest('[data-event-id]');
      if (!entry) return;
      closeList();
      if (entry.dataset.eventId === 'first-token') {
        refresh();
        overlay.hidden = false;
        overlay.querySelector('.first-token-close').focus();
      } else if (entry.dataset.eventId === 'arena') {
        toggle.focus();
        window.openArena?.();
      }
    });
    overlay.querySelector('.first-token-close').addEventListener('click', close);
    overlay.addEventListener('click', event => { if (event.target === overlay) close(); });
    overlay.querySelector('.first-token-zoom').addEventListener('click', () => {
      close();
      window.openFleetShipDetailByName?.('battlecruiser', '거북선');
    });
    buy.addEventListener('click', () => {
      const result = window.purchaseFirstTokenEvent?.();
      if (!result?.ok) {
        if (result?.reason === '사자증표가 부족합니다.') shortage.hidden = false;
        else status.textContent = result?.reason || '구매를 완료하지 못했습니다.';
        return;
      }
      refresh();
    });
    document.addEventListener('keydown', event => { if (event.key === 'Escape') { closeList(); close(); shortage.hidden = true; } });
    window.addEventListener('game-resources-updated', refresh);
    refresh();
  });
})();

/* ===== FILE: game-preferences.js ===== */
(() => {
  'use strict';
  const KEY='starfleet-preferences-v1', ID_KEY='starfleet-player-id-v1';
  const read=key=>{try{return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key)||'null');}catch{return null;}};
  const clamp=value=>Math.min(1,Math.max(0,Number(value)||0));
  const saved=read(KEY)||{};
  const channel=(value,volume)=>({enabled:value?.enabled!==false,volume:Number.isFinite(value?.volume)?clamp(value.volume):volume});
  let state={music:channel(saved.music,.65),effects:channel(saved.effects,1),language:saved.language==='en'?'en':'ko',accountProvider:['guest','google','apple'].includes(saved.accountProvider)?saved.accountProvider:'guest'};
  let playerId=read(ID_KEY);
  if(typeof playerId!=='string'||!/^SF-[A-F0-9-]{32,36}$/.test(playerId)){
    playerId='SF-'+(crypto.randomUUID?crypto.randomUUID():Array.from(crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(16)),byte=>byte.toString(16).padStart(2,'0')).join('')).toUpperCase();
    try{localStorage.setItem(ID_KEY,JSON.stringify(playerId));}catch{}
  }
  const volumeProperty=Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(HTMLMediaElement.prototype,'volume');
  const mutedProperty=Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(HTMLMediaElement.prototype,'muted');
  const voices=new Set();
  const register=(audio,group,initialVolume=1,initialMuted=false)=>{
    let baseVolume=initialVolume,baseMuted=initialMuted;
    const apply=()=>{
      const preference=state[group];
      volumeProperty.set.call(audio,baseVolume*preference.volume);
      mutedProperty.set.call(audio,baseMuted||!preference.enabled);
    };
    // Engine reads/writes retain the original per-sound mix and priority ducking.
    Object.defineProperties(audio,{
      volume:{configurable:true,get:()=>baseVolume,set:value=>{baseVolume=clamp(value);apply();}},
      muted:{configurable:true,get:()=>baseMuted,set:value=>{baseMuted=Boolean(value);apply();}}
    });
    const clone=audio.cloneNode.bind(audio);
    audio.cloneNode=deep=>register(clone(deep),group,baseVolume,baseMuted);
    audio.applyGameVolume=apply;
    voices.add(new WeakRef(audio));apply();
    return audio;
  };
  window.createGameAudio=(source,group='effects')=>register(new Audio(source),group==='music'?'music':'effects');
  window.gamePreferences={
    playerId,version:'개발 프로토타입 · 2026.10.03',
    get:()=>JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(state)),
    update(patch){
      if(patch.music)state.music=channel({...state.music,...patch.music},.65);
      if(patch.effects)state.effects=channel({...state.effects,...patch.effects},1);
      if(['ko','en'].includes(patch.language))state.language=patch.language;
      if(['guest','google','apple'].includes(patch.accountProvider))state.accountProvider=patch.accountProvider;
      let persisted=true;
      try{localStorage.setItem(KEY,JSON.stringify(state));}catch{persisted=false;}
      for(const ref of voices){const audio=ref.deref();if(audio)audio.applyGameVolume();else voices.delete(ref);}
      window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('game-preferences-changed',{detail:this.get()}));
      return persisted;
    }
  };
  const music=window.createGameAudio('sound/battlecruiser_mainBGM.mp3','music');
  music.preload='none';music.loop=true;music.volume=.35;
  const battleMusic=window.createGameAudio('sound/battle_bgm.mp3','music');
  battleMusic.preload='auto';battleMusic.loop=true;battleMusic.volume=.42;
  const battleBossMusic=window.createGameAudio('sound/battle_boss_bgm.mp3','music');
  battleBossMusic.preload='auto';battleBossMusic.loop=true;battleBossMusic.volume=.48;
  let unlocked=false;
  let bossBattleMusic=false;
  const syncMusic=()=>{
    const battleScreen=document.querySelector('.screen.active')?.id==='battle';
    const selectedMusic=battleScreen?(bossBattleMusic?battleBossMusic:battleMusic):music;
    [music,battleMusic,battleBossMusic].forEach(track=>{if(track!==selectedMusic)track.pause();});
    if(!unlocked||document.hidden||!state.music.enabled||state.music.volume===0){selectedMusic.pause();return;}
    selectedMusic.play().catch(()=>{});
  };
  window.setBattleBossMusic=enabled=>{bossBattleMusic=Boolean(enabled);syncMusic();};
  const unlock=()=>{unlocked=true;syncMusic();};
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown',unlock,{once:true});
  document.addEventListener('keydown',unlock,{once:true});
  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',syncMusic);
  window.addEventListener('game-preferences-changed',syncMusic);
  window.addEventListener('game-screen-changed',syncMusic);
})();

/* ===== FILE: ladder-ranking.js ===== */
(() => {
  const categories = [
    {
      id: 'total', label: '총전투력', code: 'TOTAL POWER', metric: '전투력', rank: 128, value: 482600,
      leaders: [['NOVA PRIME', 982400], ['청룡 함대', 941200], ['SIRIUS-01', 915800], ['카이론', 876500], ['Admiral K', 842300]]
    },
    {
      id: 'dreadnought', label: '중전함', code: 'DREADNOUGHT', metric: '함급 전투력', rank: 76, value: 98420,
      leaders: [['IRON CROWN', 186700], ['백두산', 178250], ['Vanguard-9', 170880], ['묵룡', 166300], ['ARES FLEET', 160940]]
    },
    {
      id: 'battlecruiser', label: '배틀크루저', code: 'BATTLECRUISER', metric: '함급 전투력', rank: 214, value: 72880,
      leaders: [['Solaris', 194600], ['이클립스', 187500], ['ORBITAL-7', 181420], ['은하수', 176900], ['VEGA ACE', 169300]]
    },
    {
      id: 'destroyer', label: '구축함', code: 'DESTROYER', metric: '함급 전투력', rank: 51, value: 85210,
      leaders: [['RED COMET', 151800], ['충무공', 146700], ['POLARIS', 142460], ['검은 파도', 138900], ['NEMESIS', 134280]]
    },
    {
      id: 'frigate', label: '프리깃', code: 'FRIGATE', metric: '함급 전투력', rank: 342, value: 39450,
      leaders: [['SWIFT-01', 112900], ['해오라기', 108550], ['BLUE SPEAR', 104800], ['라그랑주', 101240], ['MISTRAL', 98900]]
    },
    {
      id: 'alliance', label: '연합', code: 'ALLIANCE', metric: '연합 명성', rank: 18, value: 12680, selfLabel: '우리 연합 · 오리온',
      leaders: [['TERRA UNION', 28450], ['은하 방위군', 27120], ['SOL GUARD', 26380], ['대한 함대', 24960], ['NEBULA CORPS', 23610]]
    },
    {
      id: 'arena', label: '아레나', code: 'ARENA', metric: '시즌 점수', rank: 93, value: 2140,
      leaders: [['PHOTON KING', 4820], ['무적함대', 4650], ['ACE-77', 4515], ['STARBLADE', 4380], ['아틀라스', 4260]]
    }
  ];

  const rewardTiers = [
    { max: 1, label: '1위', tier: '챔피언', gold: 30000, thorium: 120 },
    { max: 10, label: '2–10위', tier: '마스터', gold: 20000, thorium: 80 },
    { max: 100, label: '11–100위', tier: '엘리트', gold: 10000, thorium: 40 },
    { max: 500, label: '101–500위', tier: '베테랑', gold: 5000, thorium: 15 }
  ];

  let activeCategory = 'total';
  let opener = null;
  const claimed = new Set();
  const balances = window.gameResources || (window.gameResources = { gold: 1240, thorium: 86 });
  const format = value => Number(value).toLocaleString('ko-KR');
  const rewardFor = rank => rewardTiers.find(tier => rank <= tier.max) || { label: '참가', tier: '도전자', gold: 2000, thorium: 5 };

  const updateResourceHud = () => {
    document.querySelectorAll('.fixed-top .resources').forEach(resources => {
      const items = resources.querySelectorAll('.resource');
      if (items[0]) items[0].dataset.value = format(balances.gold);
      if (items[1]) items[1].dataset.value = format(balances.thorium);
    });
    window.dispatchEvent(new Event('game-resources-updated'));
  };
  window.updateGameResourceHud = updateResourceHud;

  const rowMarkup = (rank, name, value, category, isSelf = false) => {
    const podium = rank <= 3 ? ` podium-${rank}` : '';
    return `<div class="ladder-row${podium}${isSelf ? ' is-self' : ''}">
      <span class="ladder-rank">${rank}</span>
      <span class="ladder-player"><b>${name}</b><small>${isSelf ? 'MY RANKING' : 'COMMANDER'}</small></span>
      <span class="ladder-score"><b>${format(value)}</b><small>${category.metric}</small></span>
    </div>`;
  };

  const createModal = () => {
    if (document.getElementById('ladder-modal')) return;
    const modal = document.createElement('div');
    modal.id = 'ladder-modal';
    modal.className = 'ladder-modal';
    modal.hidden = true;
    modal.innerHTML = `
      <div class="ladder-backdrop"></div>
      <article class="ladder-panel" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="ladder-title">
        <header class="ladder-header">
          <div><small>SEASON 01 · SOL FRONT</small><h2 id="ladder-title">LADDER</h2></div>
          <div class="ladder-season"><span>종료까지</span><b>06D 18H</b></div>
          <button class="ladder-close" type="button" aria-label="Ladder 닫기">×</button>
        </header>
        <div class="ladder-tabs" role="tablist" aria-label="랭킹 분야"></div>
        <section class="ladder-summary" aria-live="polite"></section>
        <section class="ladder-board" aria-label="순위표"></section>
        <section class="ladder-rewards" aria-label="순위 보상"></section>
      </article>`;
    document.body.append(modal);
    modal.querySelector('.ladder-backdrop').addEventListener('click', closeLadder);
    modal.querySelector('.ladder-close').addEventListener('click', closeLadder);
    modal.querySelector('.ladder-tabs').addEventListener('click', event => {
      const tab = event.target.closest('[data-ladder-category]');
      if (!tab) return;
      if(tab.dataset.ladderCategory==='arena'){closeLadder();window.openArena?.();return;}
      activeCategory = tab.dataset.ladderCategory;
      renderLadder();
    });
    modal.querySelector('.ladder-tabs').addEventListener('keydown', event => {
      if (!['ArrowLeft', 'ArrowRight'].includes(event.key)) return;
      event.preventDefault();
      const index = categories.findIndex(category => category.id === activeCategory);
      const direction = event.key === 'ArrowRight' ? 1 : -1;
      activeCategory = categories[(index + direction + categories.length) % categories.length].id;
      if(activeCategory==='arena'){closeLadder();window.openArena?.();return;}
      renderLadder();
      modal.querySelector(`[data-ladder-category="${activeCategory}"]`)?.focus();
    });
  };

  const renderLadder = () => {
    const modal = document.getElementById('ladder-modal');
    if (!modal) return;
    const category = categories.find(item => item.id === activeCategory) || categories[0];
    const reward = rewardFor(category.rank);
    modal.querySelector('.ladder-tabs').innerHTML = categories.map(item => `<button type="button" role="tab" data-ladder-category="${item.id}" aria-selected="${item.id === category.id}" class="${item.id === category.id ? 'selected' : ''}">${item.label}</button>`).join('');
    modal.querySelector('.ladder-summary').innerHTML = `<div><small>${category.code}</small><b>내 순위 <em>${format(category.rank)}위</em></b></div><div><small>${category.metric}</small><strong>${format(category.value)}</strong></div>`;
    modal.querySelector('.ladder-board').innerHTML = `<div class="ladder-columns"><span>RANK</span><span>PLAYER</span><span>SCORE</span></div>${category.leaders.map(([name, value], index) => rowMarkup(index + 1, name, value, category)).join('')}<div class="ladder-separator"><span>•••</span></div>${rowMarkup(category.rank, category.selfLabel || '나 · 지휘관 리안', category.value, category, true)}`;
    modal.querySelector('.ladder-rewards').innerHTML = `<div class="ladder-reward-head"><div><small>SEASON REWARD</small><b>${reward.tier} 보상</b></div><span>${reward.label}</span></div><div class="ladder-reward-track">${rewardTiers.map(tier => `<div class="${tier === reward ? 'current-tier' : ''}"><b>${tier.label}</b><span><i class="ladder-resource-icon gold"></i>${format(tier.gold)}</span><span><i class="ladder-resource-icon thorium"></i>${tier.thorium}</span></div>`).join('')}</div><button class="ladder-claim" type="button" ${claimed.has(category.id) ? 'disabled' : ''}>${claimed.has(category.id) ? '수령 완료' : '현재 순위 보상 수령'}</button>`;
    const claimButton = modal.querySelector('.ladder-claim');
    if (!claimButton.disabled) claimButton.addEventListener('click', () => {
      claimed.add(category.id);
      balances.gold += reward.gold;
      balances.thorium += reward.thorium;
      updateResourceHud();
      window.notice?.(`${category.label} 보상 획득 · 골드 ${format(reward.gold)} / 토륨 ${reward.thorium}`);
      renderLadder();
    });
  };

  function closeLadder() {
    const modal = document.getElementById('ladder-modal');
    if (!modal || modal.hidden) return;
    modal.hidden = true;
    document.body.classList.remove('ladder-open');
    opener?.focus();
  }

  window.openLadder = (category = 'total') => {
    if(category==='arena'){window.openArena?.();return;}
    activeCategory = categories.some(item => item.id === category) ? category : 'total';
    createModal();
    opener = document.activeElement;
    const modal = document.getElementById('ladder-modal');
    modal.hidden = false;
    document.body.classList.add('ladder-open');
    renderLadder();
    modal.querySelector('.ladder-close')?.focus();
  };

  document.addEventListener('keydown', event => {
    if (event.key === 'Escape') closeLadder();
  });
  window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', updateResourceHud);
})();

/* ===== FILE: mars-scenario.js ===== */
(()=>{
  'use strict';
  const KEY='starfleet-mars-scenario-v1';
  const PROFILE_KEY='starfleet-commander-profile-v1';
  const EVA_KEY='starfleet-eva9-tutorial-v1';
  const RESOURCE_KEY='starfleet-game-resources-v1';
  const LOOT_KEY='starfleet-exploration-loot-v1';
  const read=key=>{try{return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key)||'null');}catch{return null;}};
  let state={stage:'',index:0,gate6Done:false,gate9Done:false,pendingGate:0,...(read(KEY)||{})};
  let overlay;
  const save=()=>{try{localStorage.setItem(KEY,JSON.stringify(state));}catch{}};
  const evaActive=()=>{const step=read(EVA_KEY)?.step;return !!step&&step!=='complete';};
  const captain=()=>{const profile=read(PROFILE_KEY)||{};return {name:[profile.character,profile.lastName].filter(Boolean).join(' ')||'함장',image:profile.character==='Donna'?'icons/captain-donna-portrait.png':'icons/captain-jake-portrait.png'};};
  const gate6Lines=[
    {speaker:'han',name:'한성민 제독',text:'자네가 새로 임관한 함장이군. 환영인사 할 새도 없이 작전에 투입하게 되어 미안하지만, 지금은 화성의 상황이 심상치 않으니 상황이 정리되는 대로 한 번 보도록 하지. 수고해주게.'},
    {speaker:'eva',name:'EVA-9',text:'화성 인근에서 다수의 생체 신호가 감지되고 있습니다. 새로운 생체신호도 감지되고 있으니 주의하십시오. 전투를 통해 자원을 획득하시면 연구소에서 함대 강화하는 것을 잊으시면 안됩니다. 그럼, 무운을 빌겠습니다.'},
    {speaker:'captain',name:'함장',text:'어, AI가 그런것도 하나?'},
    {speaker:'eva',name:'EVA-9',text:'인간에게 친화적인 응대에 관한 코딩의 결과일 뿐입니다.'}
  ];
  const gate9Line={speaker:'han',name:'한성민 제독',text:'화성의 다른 지역은 모두 정리 되었다. 자네가 있는 쪽에 다른 생체 반응이 있다고 하니, 주의해서 확인하도록. 이상.'};
  const titanBriefingLines=[
    {speaker:'han',name:'한성민 제독',text:()=>`배치되자마자 성과를 올렸군, ${captain().name}.`},
    {speaker:'han',name:'한성민 제독',text:'자네에겐 미안하지만 타이탄의 전진기지에서 다수의 생명체 반응이 나타났다고 도움을 요청했네.'},
    {speaker:'han',name:'한성민 제독',text:'사령부의 첩보에 따르면 태양계 밖에서 타이탄으로 이동하는 더 많은 개체가 있다는 군.'},
    {speaker:'han',name:'한성민 제독',text:'이쪽은 정리가 다 되었으니 즉시 타이탄 기지로 향해주게.'},
    {speaker:'han',name:'한성민 제독',text:'그럼..'}
  ];
  const active=()=>!!state.stage;
  const current=()=>state.stage==='call'?{speaker:'eva',name:'EVA-9',text:'함장님, 제독님께서 찾으십니다. 함교로 이동해주세요.'}:state.stage==='briefing'?gate6Lines[Math.min(state.index,gate6Lines.length-1)]:state.stage==='warning'?gate9Line:state.stage==='titan-briefing'?titanBriefingLines[Math.min(state.index,titanBriefingLines.length-1)]:null;
  const portraitFor=speaker=>speaker==='han'?'han-seong-min-bust.png':speaker==='captain'?captain().image:'eva9-tutorial-bust.png';
  const paint=()=>{
    if(!overlay)return;
    const line=current();overlay.hidden=!line;document.body.dataset.marsScene=line?state.stage:'';
    if(!line)return;
    overlay.dataset.speaker=line.speaker;
    overlay.querySelector('.mars-scenario-portrait').src=portraitFor(line.speaker);
    overlay.querySelector('.mars-scenario-portrait').alt=`${line.speaker==='captain'?captain().name:line.name} 흉상`;
    overlay.querySelector('.mars-scenario-kicker').textContent=state.stage==='call'?'MARS · GATE 06 / MISSION COMPLETE':state.stage==='warning'?'MARS · GATE 09 / COMMAND TRANSMISSION':state.stage==='titan-briefing'?'TITAN · SCENARIO 02 / COMMAND BRIEFING':'UNITED STAR FLEET · BRIDGE';
    overlay.querySelector('.mars-scenario-name').textContent=line.speaker==='captain'?captain().name:line.name;
    overlay.querySelector('.mars-scenario-text').textContent=typeof line.text==='function'?line.text():line.text;
    const lineCount=state.stage==='briefing'?gate6Lines.length:state.stage==='titan-briefing'?titanBriefingLines.length:1;
    overlay.querySelector('.mars-scenario-next').textContent=state.stage==='call'?'함교':state.index<lineCount-1?'계속':'확인';
    overlay.querySelector('.mars-scenario-next').focus({preventScroll:true});
  };
  const setStage=(stage,index=0)=>{state.stage=stage;state.index=index;save();paint();};
  const finish=()=>{
    if(state.stage==='briefing')state.gate6Done=true;
    if(state.stage==='warning')state.gate9Done=true;
    if(state.stage==='titan-briefing')state.titanIntroDone=true;
    state.pendingTitanIntro=false;
    setStage('');
  };
  const grantTitanIntroRewards=()=>{
    if(state.titanRewardGranted)return;
    const balances={gold:1240,thorium:86,...(read(RESOURCE_KEY)||{}),...(window.gameResources||{})};
    const loot=read(LOOT_KEY)||{};
    balances.gold=Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(balances.gold)||0))+10000;
    loot.scoutTron=Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(loot.scoutTron)||0))+10;
    loot.researchStaff=Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(loot.researchStaff)||0))+10;
    try{
      localStorage.setItem(RESOURCE_KEY,JSON.stringify(balances));
      localStorage.setItem(LOOT_KEY,JSON.stringify(loot));
      window.gameResources=balances;
      state.titanRewardGranted=true;save();
      window.updateGameResourceHud?.();
      window.dispatchEvent(new Event('game-resources-updated'));
    }catch{}
  };
  const beginTitanBriefing=()=>{
    if(active()||state.titanIntroDone)return;
    state.pendingTitanIntro=false;save();
    window.show?.('home');
    setStage('titan-briefing');
  };
  const advance=()=>{
    if(state.stage==='call'){
      // The bridge is the only permitted destination while EVA-9 calls the captain.
      setStage('briefing');
      window.show?.('home');
      paint();
    }else if(state.stage==='briefing'){
      if(state.index<gate6Lines.length-1)setStage('briefing',state.index+1);
      else finish();
    }else if(state.stage==='titan-briefing'){
      if(state.index<titanBriefingLines.length-1)setStage('titan-briefing',state.index+1);
      else finish();
    }else if(state.stage==='warning')finish();
  };
  const begin=(gate)=>{
    if(active())return;
    if(evaActive()){state.pendingGate=gate;save();return;}
    state.pendingGate=0;save();
    if(gate===6&&!state.gate6Done)setStage('call');
    if(gate===9&&!state.gate9Done)setStage('warning');
  };
  const previousAllowsScreen=window.tutorialAllowsScreen;
  window.tutorialAllowsScreen=id=>{
    if(previousAllowsScreen&&!previousAllowsScreen(id))return false;
    if(!active())return true;
    return state.stage==='briefing'&&id==='home';
  };
  window.addEventListener('campaign-gate-cleared',event=>{
    const {chapter,region,gate,firstClear}=event.detail||{};
    if(Number(chapter)!==1||region!=='mars'||!firstClear)return;
    if(Number(gate)===6||Number(gate)===9)begin(Number(gate));
    if(Number(gate)===10&&!state.titanIntroDone){
      grantTitanIntroRewards();
      if(evaActive()){state.pendingTitanIntro=true;save();}
      else beginTitanBriefing();
    }
  });
  window.addEventListener('eva9-tutorial-completed',()=>{if(state.pendingTitanIntro)beginTitanBriefing();else if(state.pendingGate)begin(Number(state.pendingGate));});
  document.addEventListener('click',event=>{
    if(!active()||event.target.closest('.mars-scenario'))return;
    event.preventDefault();event.stopImmediatePropagation();
  },true);
  window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>{
    overlay=document.createElement('div');
    overlay.className='mars-scenario';overlay.hidden=true;
    overlay.setAttribute('role','dialog');overlay.setAttribute('aria-modal','true');overlay.setAttribute('aria-label','화성 작전 시나리오');
    overlay.innerHTML='<div class="mars-scenario-card"><img class="mars-scenario-portrait" alt=""/><div class="mars-scenario-dialogue"><small class="mars-scenario-kicker"></small><strong class="mars-scenario-name"></strong><p class="mars-scenario-text"></p><div class="mars-scenario-actions"><button type="button" class="mars-scenario-next"></button></div></div></div>';
    document.body.append(overlay);
    overlay.querySelector('.mars-scenario-next').addEventListener('click',advance);
    overlay.addEventListener('click',event=>{if(event.target===overlay)event.preventDefault();});
    if(state.stage==='call'||state.stage==='briefing'||state.stage==='titan-briefing'){
      // A reload cannot restore the completed battle result; resume safely on the bridge.
      if(state.stage==='titan-briefing')state.index=Math.max(0,Math.min(titanBriefingLines.length-1,Number(state.index)||0));
      else{state.stage='briefing';state.index=Math.max(0,Math.min(gate6Lines.length-1,Number(state.index)||0));}
      save();
      setTimeout(()=>{window.show?.('home');paint();},120);
    }else if(state.stage==='warning')paint();
    else if(state.pendingTitanIntro&&!evaActive())setTimeout(beginTitanBriefing,120);
    else if(state.pendingGate&&!evaActive())setTimeout(()=>begin(Number(state.pendingGate)),120);
  });
})();

/* ===== FILE: mission-board.js ===== */
(() => {
  const categories = [
    {
      id: 'daily', label: 'DAILY', code: 'DAILY OPERATIONS', reset: '갱신 13H 42M',

        { id: 'veteran', icon: '★', title: '백전노장', detail: '전투 100회 완료', progress: 37, goal: 100, reward: { gold: 10000, thorium: 30 } },
        { id: 'collector', icon: '▦', title: '함선 수집가', detail: '서로 다른 함선 20종 획득', progress: 14, goal: 20, reward: { gold: 8000, thorium: 25 } },
        { id: 'legend', icon: '♛', title: '전설의 기함', detail: 'L급 함선 1척 획득', progress: 1, goal: 1, reward: { gold: 12000, thorium: 40 } },
        { id: 'max-level', icon: '▲', title: '극한 강화', detail: '함선 레벨 50 달성', progress: 32, goal: 50, reward: { gold: 15000, thorium: 45 } },
        { id: 'weapons', icon: '⌖', title: '무장 전문가', detail: '무기 50종 획득', progress: 18, goal: 50, reward: { gold: 10000, thorium: 30 } },
        { id: 'alliance', icon: '⬡', title: '연합의 일원', detail: '연합 가입', progress: 1, goal: 1, reward: { gold: 2500, thorium: 10 } },
        { id: 'arena', icon: '♜', title: '아레나 도전자', detail: '아레나 전투 100회 참가', progress: 24, goal: 100, reward: { gold: 12000, thorium: 35 } },
        { id: 'explorer', icon: '◉', title: '은하 개척자', detail: '작전 구역 10개 해방', progress: 4, goal: 10, reward: { gold: 16000, thorium: 50 } },
        { id: 'wealth', icon: '▰', title: '은하의 자산가', detail: '골드 누적 1,000,000 획득', progress: 482600, goal: 1000000, reward: { gold: 25000, thorium: 75 } }
      ],
      milestones: [
        { goal: 2, gold: 5000, thorium: 20 },
        { goal: 5, gold: 15000, thorium: 60 },
        { goal: 10, gold: 30000, thorium: 120 }
      ]
    }
  ];

  let activeCategory = 'daily';
  let opener = null;
  const claimedMissions = new Set();
  const claimedMilestones = new Set();
  const format = value => Number(value).toLocaleString('ko-KR');
  const isComplete = mission => mission.progress >= mission.goal;
  const categoryFor = id => categories.find(category => category.id === id) || categories[0];
  const rewardMarkup = reward => `<span><i class="mission-resource-icon gold" aria-hidden="true"></i>${format(reward.gold)}</span><span><i class="mission-resource-icon thorium" aria-hidden="true"></i>${reward.thorium}</span>`;

  const updateResources = reward => {
    const balances = window.gameResources || (window.gameResources = { gold: 1240, thorium: 86 });
    balances.gold += reward.gold;
    balances.thorium += reward.thorium;
    if (window.updateGameResourceHud) {
      window.updateGameResourceHud();
      return;
    }
    document.querySelectorAll('.fixed-top .resources').forEach(resources => {
      const items = resources.querySelectorAll('.resource');
      if (items[0]) items[0].dataset.value = format(balances.gold);
      if (items[1]) items[1].dataset.value = format(balances.thorium);
    });
  };

  const syncDeckProgress = () => {
    const daily = categoryFor('daily');
    const completed = daily.missions.filter(isComplete).length;
    const counter = document.querySelector('.mission-button em');
    if (counter) counter.textContent = `${completed} / ${daily.missions.length}`;
  };

  const missionMarkup = (mission, category) => {
    const complete = isComplete(mission);
    const claimKey = `${category.id}:${mission.id}`;
    const claimed = claimedMissions.has(claimKey);
    const percent = Math.min(100, Math.round((mission.progress / mission.goal) * 100));
    const buttonLabel = claimed ? '수령 완료' : complete ? '보상 수령' : '진행 중';
    return `<article class="mission-item${complete ? ' is-complete' : ''}${claimed ? ' is-claimed' : ''}">
      <span class="mission-symbol" aria-hidden="true">${mission.icon}</span>
      <div class="mission-info">
        <header><div><b>${mission.title}</b><small>${mission.detail}</small></div><strong>${format(Math.min(mission.progress, mission.goal))} / ${format(mission.goal)}</strong></header>
        <div class="mission-progress" aria-label="진행률 ${percent}%"><i style="width:${percent}%"></i></div>
        <div class="mission-reward"><small>COMPLETION REWARD</small>${rewardMarkup(mission.reward)}</div>
      </div>
      <button class="mission-claim" type="button" data-mission-claim="${mission.id}" ${complete && !claimed ? '' : 'disabled'}>${buttonLabel}</button>
    </article>`;
  };

  const milestoneMarkup = (milestone, index, category, completed) => {
    const key = `${category.id}:${milestone.goal}`;
    const available = completed >= milestone.goal;
    const claimed = claimedMilestones.has(key);
    const state = claimed ? 'is-claimed' : available ? 'is-ready' : '';
    const label = claimed ? '완료' : available ? '수령' : `${completed}/${milestone.goal}`;
    return `<article class="mission-milestone ${state}">
      <span class="mission-milestone-number">0${index + 1}</span>
      <div><small>MILESTONE</small><b>${milestone.goal}개 달성</b><p>${rewardMarkup(milestone)}</p></div>
      <button type="button" data-milestone-claim="${milestone.goal}" ${available && !claimed ? '' : 'disabled'}>${label}</button>
    </article>`;
  };

  const createModal = () => {
    if (document.getElementById('mission-modal')) return;
    const modal = document.createElement('div');
    modal.id = 'mission-modal';
    modal.className = 'mission-modal';
    modal.hidden = true;
    modal.innerHTML = `
      <div class="mission-backdrop"></div>
      <article class="mission-panel" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="mission-title">
        <header class="mission-header">
          <div class="mission-header-mark" aria-hidden="true">M</div>
          <div><small>ORION FLEET COMMAND</small><h2 id="mission-title">MISSION CONTROL</h2></div>
          <div class="mission-reset"><span>작전 기록</span><b>03 / 07</b></div>
          <button class="mission-close" type="button" aria-label="Mission 닫기">×</button>
        </header>
        <div class="mission-tabs" role="tablist" aria-label="미션 분류"></div>
        <section class="mission-document">
          <header class="mission-document-head"></header>
          <section class="mission-milestones" aria-label="마일스톤 보상"></section>
          <section class="mission-list" aria-label="미션 목록"></section>
        </section>
      </article>`;
    document.body.append(modal);
    modal.querySelector('.mission-backdrop').addEventListener('click', closeMissionBoard);
    modal.querySelector('.mission-close').addEventListener('click', closeMissionBoard);
    modal.querySelector('.mission-tabs').addEventListener('click', event => {
      const tab = event.target.closest('[data-mission-category]');
      if (!tab) return;
      activeCategory = tab.dataset.missionCategory;
      renderMissionBoard();
    });
    modal.querySelector('.mission-tabs').addEventListener('keydown', event => {
      if (!['ArrowLeft', 'ArrowRight'].includes(event.key)) return;
      event.preventDefault();
      const index = categories.findIndex(category => category.id === activeCategory);
      const direction = event.key === 'ArrowRight' ? 1 : -1;
      activeCategory = categories[(index + direction + categories.length) % categories.length].id;
      renderMissionBoard();
      modal.querySelector(`[data-mission-category="${activeCategory}"]`)?.focus();
    });
    modal.querySelector('.mission-document').addEventListener('click', event => {
      const category = categoryFor(activeCategory);
      const missionButton = event.target.closest('[data-mission-claim]');
      if (missionButton) {
        const mission = category.missions.find(item => item.id === missionButton.dataset.missionClaim);
        if (!mission || !isComplete(mission)) return;
        const key = `${category.id}:${mission.id}`;
        if (claimedMissions.has(key)) return;
        claimedMissions.add(key);
        updateResources(mission.reward);
        window.notice?.(`${mission.title} 보상 획득 · 골드 ${format(mission.reward.gold)} / 토륨 ${mission.reward.thorium}`);
        renderMissionBoard();
        return;
      }
      const milestoneButton = event.target.closest('[data-milestone-claim]');
      if (!milestoneButton) return;
      const goal = Number(milestoneButton.dataset.milestoneClaim);
      const milestone = category.milestones.find(item => item.goal === goal);
      const completed = category.missions.filter(isComplete).length;
      const key = `${category.id}:${goal}`;
      if (!milestone || completed < goal || claimedMilestones.has(key)) return;
      claimedMilestones.add(key);
      updateResources(milestone);
      window.notice?.(`${category.label} 마일스톤 보상 획득 · 골드 ${format(milestone.gold)} / 토륨 ${milestone.thorium}`);
      renderMissionBoard();
    });
  };

  const renderMissionBoard = () => {
    const modal = document.getElementById('mission-modal');
    if (!modal) return;
    const category = categoryFor(activeCategory);
    const completed = category.missions.filter(isComplete).length;
    modal.querySelector('.mission-tabs').innerHTML = categories.map(item => `<button type="button" role="tab" data-mission-category="${item.id}" aria-selected="${item.id === category.id}" class="${item.id === category.id ? 'selected' : ''}"><span>${item.label}</span><small>${item.missions.filter(isComplete).length}/${item.missions.length}</small></button>`).join('');
    modal.querySelector('.mission-document-head').innerHTML = `<div><small>${category.code}</small><b>${category.label === 'ACHIEVEMENT' ? '업적 기록' : '작전 명령서'}</b></div><div><span>${category.reset}</span><strong>${completed}<em> / ${category.missions.length}</em></strong></div>`;
    modal.querySelector('.mission-milestones').innerHTML = category.milestones.map((milestone, index) => milestoneMarkup(milestone, index, category, completed)).join('');
    modal.querySelector('.mission-list').innerHTML = category.missions.map(mission => missionMarkup(mission, category)).join('');
    modal.querySelector('.mission-reset b').textContent = `${String(completed).padStart(2, '0')} / ${String(category.missions.length).padStart(2, '0')}`;
  };

  function closeMissionBoard() {
    const modal = document.getElementById('mission-modal');
    if (!modal || modal.hidden) return;
    modal.hidden = true;
    document.body.classList.remove('mission-open');
    opener?.focus();
  }

  window.openMissionBoard = () => {
    createModal();
    opener = document.activeElement;
    const modal = document.getElementById('mission-modal');
    modal.hidden = false;
    document.body.classList.add('mission-open');
    renderMissionBoard();
    modal.querySelector('.mission-close')?.focus();
  };

  document.addEventListener('keydown', event => {
    if (event.key === 'Escape') closeMissionBoard();
  });
  window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', syncDeckProgress);
})();

/* ===== FILE: ship-detail.js ===== */

      <button class="detail-back" onclick="show('home')">← 함교</button>

      <div class="ship-status"><b>전투력 1,280</b><span>READY FOR DEPLOYMENT</span></div>

/* ===== FILE: summon-enhancement.js ===== */
window.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
  const tabs = document.querySelector('.summon-tabs');
  const weaponPool = document.getElementById('weapon-pool');
  if (!tabs || !weaponPool || document.getElementById('captain-pool')) return;
  const weaponImage = weaponPool.querySelector('.weapon-art img');
  if (weaponImage) weaponImage.remove();
  const weaponTitle = weaponPool.querySelector(':scope > b');
  if (weaponTitle) weaponTitle.textContent = '전술 무기 연구';

  const tacticalWeaponStorageKey = 'starfleet-tactical-weapon-research-v1';
  let tacticalWeaponState = {};
  try { tacticalWeaponState = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(tacticalWeaponStorageKey) || '{}') || {}; } catch { tacticalWeaponState = {}; }
  let researchJournal = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(tacticalWeaponState.researchJournal) || 0));
  let researchCore = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(tacticalWeaponState.researchCore) || 0));
  const tacticalFields = [
    ['cannon', '함포', '⚓', '포신 합금'],
    ['missile', '미사일', '◈', '유도 회로'],
    ['electric', '전기', 'ϟ', '전도 코어'],
    ['laser', '레이저포', '⌁', '광학 렌즈'],
    ['piercing', '관통레이저', '╫', '집속 결정'],
    ['carrier', '함재기', '✈', '비행 제어칩'],
    ['defense', '방어모듈', '⬡', '차폐 합금'],
    ['frigate', '프리깃', '⌬', '지원 프로토콜']
  ];
  const tacticalBadge = (id, fallback, context) => {
    if (id === 'cannon') return '<svg class="tactical-weapon-icon tactical-cannon-icon" viewBox="0 0 32 32" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M4 10.5h17.5l5-2.5v6l-5-2.5H4z"/><path d="M7 13.5h13l3 7H9z"/><path d="M5 23h22"/><circle cx="11" cy="23" r="2.2"/><circle cx="22" cy="23" r="2.2"/></svg>';
    if (id === 'missile') return '<svg class="tactical-weapon-icon tactical-missile-icon" viewBox="0 0 32 32" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M16 3c4.6 4.8 6.1 10.1 5.3 18.2H10.7C9.9 13.1 11.4 7.8 16 3Z"/><path d="M10.7 16 7 21.5V26l4.7-3.4m9.6-6.6 3.7 5.5V26l-4.7-3.4M13 21.2 12.2 28 16 25l3.8 3-.8-6.8"/><circle cx="16" cy="12" r="2.1"/></svg>';
    if (id === 'laser') {
      const gradientId = `tactical-laser-gradient-${context}`;
      return `<svg class="tactical-weapon-icon tactical-laser-icon" viewBox="0 0 32 32" aria-hidden="true"><defs><linearGradient id="${gradientId}" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#ffffff"/><stop offset=".38" stop-color="#8eeeff"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#7660ff"/></linearGradient></defs><path class="laser-rail" d="M11 4 9 27m12-23 2 23"/><path class="laser-beam" d="M16 3 19.1 17 16 29l-3.1-12Z" style="fill:url(#${gradientId})"/></svg>`;
    }
    return fallback;
  };
  const saveTacticalState = () => { try { localStorage.setItem(tacticalWeaponStorageKey, JSON.stringify(tacticalWeaponState)); } catch {} window.dispatchEvent(new Event('commander-research-updated')); };
  const tacticalProgress = (id) => {
    const saved = tacticalWeaponState[id] || {};
    return { materials: Math.max(0, Number(saved.materials) || 0), level: Math.max(0, Number(saved.level) || 0) };
  };
  const dedicatedCoreCount = id => Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(tacticalWeaponState[id]?.materials) || 0));
  const coreInventoryMarkup = id => {
    const dedicated = dedicatedCoreCount(id), universal = researchCore;
    return `<span class="core-inventory-line"><img src="icons/research-core.png" alt="연구코어"/><strong>연구코어</strong><span>전용 <b>${dedicated.toLocaleString('ko-KR')}</b></span><i>/</i><span>범용 <b>${universal.toLocaleString('ko-KR')}</b></span><i>/</i><span>사용 가능 <b>${(dedicated+universal).toLocaleString('ko-KR')}</b></span></span>`;
  };
  const developmentResetCost = id => Math.min(Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER, 100 * (2 ** Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(tacticalWeaponState.developmentResetCounts?.[id]) || 0))));
  const developmentResetButton = id => `<button type="button" class="tactical-tech-reset cannon-tech-reset" data-tech-reset="${id}" title="이 분야의 개발 단계 초기화">초기화 · <b>${developmentResetCost(id).toLocaleString('ko-KR')}</b> 골드</button>`;
  const resetDevelopment = (id, modal, render) => {
    const cost=developmentResetCost(id);
    const refund=developmentSpend(id);
    let balances=window.gameResources;
    if(!balances){try{balances={gold:0,thorium:0,...(JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('starfleet-game-resources-v1')||'{}')||{})};}catch{balances={gold:0,thorium:0};}window.gameResources=balances;}
    try{const saved=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('starfleet-game-resources-v1')||'null');if(saved&&typeof saved==='object')Object.assign(balances,saved);}catch{}
    const gold=Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(balances.gold)||0));
    if(gold<cost){notice(`개발 초기화에는 골드 ${cost.toLocaleString('ko-KR')}개가 필요합니다.`);return;}
    if(!window.confirm(`${id==='cannon'?'함포':tacticalFields.find(([field])=>field===id)?.[1]||id} 개발을 초기화할까요?\n개발 단계와 적용 보너스를 초기화하고 연구일지 ${refund.journal.toLocaleString('ko-KR')}개와 범용 연구코어 ${refund.core.toLocaleString('ko-KR')}개를 반환합니다.\n비용: 골드 ${cost.toLocaleString('ko-KR')}`))return;
    balances.gold=gold-cost;window.gameResources=balances;
    researchJournal+=refund.journal;researchCore+=refund.core;saveResearchCurrency();
    try{localStorage.setItem('starfleet-game-resources-v1',JSON.stringify(balances));}catch{}
    tacticalWeaponState.developmentResetCounts={...(tacticalWeaponState.developmentResetCounts||{}),[id]:Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(tacticalWeaponState.developmentResetCounts?.[id])||0))+1};
    if(id==='cannon'){
      tacticalWeaponState.cannonTree={};
      tacticalWeaponState.cannonBonuses=cannonBonusesFromLevels({});
    }else{
      tacticalWeaponState[`${id}Tree`]={};
      tacticalWeaponState[id]={...(tacticalWeaponState[id]||{}),level:0};
    }
    saveTacticalState();window.updateGameResourceHud?.();window.dispatchEvent(new Event('game-resources-updated'));
    if(id==='frigate')window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('tactical-research-updated',{detail:{field:id,level:0}}));
    renderTacticalWeaponResearch();render();
  };
  const consumeCores = (id, amount) => {
    const dedicated = dedicatedCoreCount(id), fromDedicated = Math.min(dedicated, amount), fromUniversal = amount-fromDedicated;
    if (fromUniversal > researchCore) return false;
    if (fromDedicated) tacticalWeaponState[id] = { ...(tacticalWeaponState[id] || {}), materials: dedicated-fromDedicated };
    researchCore -= fromUniversal; saveResearchCurrency();
    return true;
  };
  const saveResearchCurrency = () => {
    tacticalWeaponState.researchJournal = researchJournal;
    tacticalWeaponState.researchCore = researchCore;
  };

  const cannonDevelopmentStage={
    firepower1:1,reload1:2,shell1:2,
    rapidFire:3,shell2:3,

  };

  const cannonDevelopmentDetail={
    firepower1:'개발 1회마다 공격속도와 데미지가 각각 0.2% 증가합니다.',
